(raised 2026-08-16, see [[01c-romans-11-remnant-grafting-and-all-israel]] §6)** **⚠️ FLAGGED SPECIFICALLY TO PREVENT ITS QUIET USE AS A RESURRECTION-TIMING DATUM.** This project has an active eschatological framework with resurrection questions in it (the millennium, the "little season," the trumpet comparison — bookmarked). **A phrase like "life from the dead" attached to Israel's acceptance is exactly the kind of material that gets absorbed into a timeline without ever being argued for.** **NOT ESTABLISHED in any direction.** Requires Greek and contextual work per ADR-0003 before any eschatological use whatsoever.

71. **CJB was NOT retrieved for Romans 11. (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01c-romans-11-remnant-grafting-and-all-israel]] §17)** **Stage 23 found THREE CJB translation divergences in a single chapter (Romans 10 — items 63, 64, and the *gē*/*oikoumenē* inversion).** Romans 11 was worked from ESV plus Greek/Hebrew/LXX retrieval only. **❗ THEIR ABSENCE IN ROMANS 11 IS UNKNOWN, NOT ESTABLISHED AS ABSENT — and no translation-divergence claim is made for this chapter in either direction.** Recorded rather than silently omitted. **Given that 11:26's *houtōs* ("in this way" vs "and then") and 11:25's *achri hou* ("until") are exactly the kind of terms English witnesses resolve differently, this is a real gap, not a formality.**

72. **What is the root of the olive tree? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01c-romans-11-remnant-grafting-and-all-israel]] §9)** **Candidates: Abraham and the patriarchal promises · the covenant promises generally · Israel's covenantal root · Messiah · some combination.** **❗ ROMANS 11 NEVER SAYS "THE ROOT IS CHRIST."** It says Gentiles **share in the nourishing root** (11:17) and that **the root supports you** (11:18). **CONTEXTUAL LEAN, RECORDED AS CONTEXTUAL NOT LEXICAL:** the Abrahamic/patriarchal framing of Romans 9–11, and especially 11:28's *"beloved for the sake of their forefathers,"* weighs toward the patriarchal-promise reading. **That is a lean, not a determination.** **Kept open deliberately because this project's own eventual identity language depends on the answer — which is precisely why it must not be settled by convenience.**

73. **What is *plērōma tōn ethnōn*, "the fullness of the Gentiles"? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01c-romans-11-remnant-grafting-and-all-israel]] §13, §23)** **Greek verified by interlinear: τὸ πλήρωμα τῶν ἐθνῶν — *plērōma* (4138) + *ethnōn* (1484).** **Candidates: a particular number · a covenantal fullness · the completion of Gentile mission · representative inclusion of the nations · something else.** **🔑 THIS TOUCHES THE ORIGINAL PROCLAMATION QUESTION DIRECTLY** — "the fullness of the nations coming in" is scope language of exactly the kind the **Universal-Language Scope Test** (Stage 23) now governs, **and it must be run through that test rather than read intuitively.** **Also interacts with §23's observation that Paul's Gentile ministry has an Israel-facing purpose (11:13–14): a first-century proclamation whose stated purpose includes provoking Israel is a different object than a generic mandate to evangelize all nations, and the two would be tested differently.** **Feeds q68.**

74. **Romans 11:26's *ek Siōn* — why does Paul match NEITHER the Hebrew NOR the Septuagint? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01c-romans-11-remnant-grafting-and-all-israel]] §14)** **THREE-WAY DIVERGENCE, ALL THREE VERIFIED BY RETRIEVAL:** **MT Isaiah 59:20** — וּבָ֤א **לְצִיּוֹן֙** גּוֹאֵ֔ל, *l'* = **TO** Zion (Sefaria) · **LXX** — καὶ ἥξει **ἕνεκεν Σιὼν** ὁ ῥυόμενος, *heneken* = **FOR THE SAKE OF** Zion (Elpenor) · **Paul, Romans 11:26** — Ἥξει **ἐκ Σιὼν** ὁ Ῥυόμενος, *ek* = **OUT OF / FROM** Zion (interlinear). **❗ THREE WITNESSES, THREE PREPOSITIONS, THREE DIRECTIONS.** **CONSTRAINT THAT FOLLOWS IMMEDIATELY: any argument resting on the DIRECTION of the Deliverer's coming — to Zion, from Zion, for Zion — rests on a preposition that differs across every witness, and this project may not build on it.** **NOT ESTABLISHED:** whether Paul used a variant Greek text now lost, quoted from memory, deliberately reshaped, or conflated Isaiah 59:20 with another passage (the covenant clause at 11:27 resembles Isaiah 27:9). **⚠️ AND NOTE THE CONTRAST WITH ITEM 65: at Romans 10:18 Paul quotes the LXX VERBATIM. Two stages, two quotations, two entirely different relationships to the source — QUOTATION BEHAVIOR MUST BE CHECKED PER QUOTATION, NOT GENERALIZED FROM THE AUTHOR.**

75. **🔑 What does "all Israel" mean in Romans 11:26? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01c-romans-11-remnant-grafting-and-all-israel]] §15)** **Greek: καὶ οὕτως πᾶς Ἰσραὴλ σωθήσεται. οὕτως verified as an ADVERB (3779) — "in this MANNER," not "and THEN." Chronology may not be imported from the English "so," which removes the sequential reading's claim to be the obvious one without establishing any other.** **FOUR READINGS, NONE SELECTED:** **(A)** ethnic/national Israel collectively, a future or climactic restoration · **(B)** the elect remnant of Israel across history · **(C)** the total covenant people, Jew + Gentile together · **(D)** a corporate-remnant formulation — Israel in its promised elect fullness rather than every ethnic Israelite without exception. **WHAT THE EVIDENCE DOES CONSTRAIN:** the **Jacob** gloss at 11:26b weighs against emptying "Israel" of ethnic reference; ***"their own olive tree"*** (11:24) weighs against replacement; ***"broken off because of unbelief"*** (11:20) weighs against ethnicity-alone. **❗ DELIBERATELY LEFT OPEN EVEN THOUGH THIS PROJECT'S IDENTITY LANGUAGE ("Messianic Israelite") WOULD BE EASIER TO JUSTIFY UNDER (C) OR (D). Selecting one here would be the Thesis Discipline failing in the hardest place to notice — where the convenient answer is also genuinely defensible.** **Load-bearing for the Stage 24 provisional synthesis; interacts with q69 and q72.**

76. **Romans 15:19's *peplērōkenai* — what does "fulfilled" mean for a mission territory? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01d-had-the-gospel-already-gone-throughout-the-world]] §6; strengthened by [[01e-did-yahweh-know-whom-the-mission-needed-to-reach]] §11)** **Greek verified by interlinear: πεπληρωκέναι, lemma πληρόω (4137), PERFECT INFINITIVE ACTIVE — a completed action with continuing resultant state.** **🔑 SAME LEMMA AS MATTHEW 5:17'S "FULFIL"** (Stage 14). Paul says that from Jerusalem around to Illyricum he has fulfilled the gospel, and that he has **"no longer any room for work in these regions" (15:23) — while millions there had never heard him.** **ESTABLISHED: apostolic mission completion does NOT require exhaustive individual evangelisation; a region can be evangelistically fulfilled in a FOUNDATIONAL sense while ordinary proclamation continues through established communities.** **⚠️ TWO BRAKES: (1) shared lemma ≠ shared meaning — *plēroō* at Matthew 5:17 (Messiah and Torah) and Romans 15:19 (Paul and a territory) do different work, and Stage 23 already showed what happens when shared lemmas get counted as independent corroborations; (2) Paul describes HIS OWN pioneering assignment, not a prophecy's fulfilment.** **Stage 25½ adds: Paul's movements were themselves divinely directed, so he did not need to know whether anyone remained to be reached THROUGH HIM — Tier B, inference from narrative pattern.**

77. **🔑 Matthew 24:3 and 24:14 — WHICH age ends, and what is *telos*? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01d-had-the-gospel-already-gone-throughout-the-world]] §11)** **Matthew 24:3 is συντέλεια τοῦ αἰῶνος — consummation of the AGE** *(verified previously, see [[00g-matthew-24-and-the-oikoumene]]; CJB renders it "that the *olam hazeh* is ending")*. **αἰών (165) ≠ κόσμος (2889) ≠ οἰκουμένη (3625) — three different words, not synonyms.** **❗ Older English renderings of 24:3 as "the end of the world" import a cosmology the Greek does not carry.** **NOT ESTABLISHED: which age the disciples understood to be ending — and that is central to preterism.** **And Matthew 24:14's "the end" is *telos* (5056), the same lexeme this project already had to brake on at Romans 10:4 (item 63): destruction of creation? end of the Mosaic covenantal order? destruction of Jerusalem? end of that eschatological crisis? final judgment? NOT ANSWERABLE FROM ENGLISH "END."** **This is the question Stage 26 (Matthew 28) may bear on directly — see item 85.**

78. **Acts 2:5's "every nation under heaven" — Greek not retrieved. (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01d-had-the-gospel-already-gone-throughout-the-world]] §8)** Luke's diaspora list (Parthians, Medes, Elamites, Mesopotamia, Judea, Cappadocia, Pontus, Asia, Phrygia, Pamphylia, Egypt, Libya, Rome, Crete, Arabia) is summarised with universal language. **Cited from the tranche; NOT verified this pass.** Relevant to the **representative reach vs exhaustive individual hearing** distinction, which may become central to the Matthew 24:14 question. **Run the Universal-Language Scope Test on it once retrieved.**

79. **Is Pentecost a deliberate Babel reversal? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01d-had-the-gospel-already-gone-throughout-the-world]] §9)** **Genesis 11: languages divided → peoples dispersed. Acts 2: many languages → one message understood → peoples gathered, then sent out.** **Tier D — INTERTEXTUAL HYPOTHESIS, NOT ESTABLISHED.** The contrast is strong; **the text does not state the connection**, and this project does not supply connections texts leave unstated. **Not necessarily a reversal of all Babel consequences.** Relevant because Matthew 24:14 concerns proclamation to the nations and Acts 2 depicts that mission beginning from Jerusalem.

80. **Acts 18:10 — what does "I have many in this city who are my people" denote about people who have not yet believed? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01e-did-yahweh-know-whom-the-mission-needed-to-reach]] §2)** **ESV verified.** Yeshua tells Paul to keep speaking *because* of these people. **The reasoning runs: Yeshua knows His people → knows where they are → keeps His messenger there → the message reaches them.** **This is the single closest text to the author's hypothesis and it is strong — and it is ONE statement, in a vision, about ONE city.** **A doctrine may not be built on it alone.**

81. **Acts 16:6–7 — on what basis were Asia and Bithynia forbidden AT THAT TIME? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01e-did-yahweh-know-whom-the-mission-needed-to-reach]] §4)** **ESV verified: "having been forbidden by the Holy Spirit to speak the word in Asia" · "the Spirit of Jesus did not allow them" [into Bithynia].** **❗ THE PROHIBITIONS ARE THE POINT — they were TRYING to proclaim and were twice stopped.** **NOT ESTABLISHED** whether the restriction was about **timing**, sequence, resource allocation, or something else. **Note Asia IS later evangelised (Acts 19), which favours a timing reading — but favouring is not establishing.**

82. **John 10:16's "other sheep" — who? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01e-did-yahweh-know-whom-the-mission-needed-to-reach]] §7)** "I have other sheep that are not of this fold. I must bring them also, and they will listen to my voice." **Gentiles is the obvious major reading — but it may not be stamped onto the text without doing the Johannine work.** Diaspora Israel is a live alternative. **⚠️ NOT RETRIEVED this pass (see item 84).** **The underlying shepherd theology is unmistakable regardless: Yeshua knows His sheep before the narrative depicts them gathered.**

83. **🔑 Acts 13:48's *tetagmenoi* — middle or passive? And should it be resolved together with Romans 9:22? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01e-did-yahweh-know-whom-the-mission-needed-to-reach]] §6)** **Greek verified by interlinear: τεταγμένοι, lemma τάσσω (5021), PERFECT PARTICIPLE — MIDDLE *OR* PASSIVE — nom. masc. plural (V-RPM/P-NMP).** **❗ THE VOICE IS GRAMMATICALLY UNDETERMINED: the form does not decide between "having been appointed" and "having arranged/disposed themselves."** **🔑 AND THIS IS THE SECOND TIME THIS PROJECT HAS VERIFIED EXACTLY THIS SITUATION — Romans 9:22's κατηρτισμένα (item 59) is also a perfect participle, middle or passive, with no stated agent.** **The two most-contested election texts in the canon both rest on a perfect participle whose voice the grammar does not settle — verified independently, both times, without importing either side of the historic debate. NEITHER SIDE CAN CLAIM THE GRAMMAR.** **That convergence is itself a finding.** **The two may need to be resolved together or not at all.** **The later Calvinist/Arminian debate is NOT imported into Acts.** The textual observation is unavoidable: Luke connects those who believed with those described as *tetagmenoi*.

85. **🔑 Is Matthew 28:20's "to the end of the age" the SAME *synteleia tou aiōnos* as Matthew 24:3? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01d-had-the-gospel-already-gone-throughout-the-world]] §16)** **VERIFY, DO NOT ASSUME.** **If they are the same phrase denoting the same age, then the Great Commission's own DURATION CLAUSE is tied to Matthew 24's framework — which would bear directly on whether Matthew 28 establishes a perpetual universal obligation or one bounded by the same horizon as Matthew 24:14.** **Potentially decisive, and the reason Stage 26 must be Matthew 28.** **Interacts with item 77 (which age?) — neither can be settled without the other.** **⚠️ AND THE BRAKE MUST RUN BOTH WAYS: identical phrasing does not by itself prove identical referent, any more than a shared lemma proved shared meaning at items 68 and 76.**

86. **🔑 REFERENTIAL half of q85 — do Matthew's four uses of συντέλεια τοῦ αἰῶνος denote ONE horizon? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01f-matthew-28-the-great-commission-in-context]] §11)** **LEXICAL HALF NOW CLOSED BY RETRIEVAL: all four are the same phrase — συντέλεια (4930) + αἰών (165) — at Matthew 13:39, 13:49, 24:3, and 28:20.** **This is a MATTHEW-DISTINCTIVE expression and the Great Commission's own duration clause is one of the four.** **Invisible in English**, where older renderings give 13:39 "end of the world" against 24:3/28:20 "end of the age." **🚧 BUT IDENTICAL PHRASING DOES NOT PROVE IDENTICAL REFERENT — the same brake that killed the Stage 23 bridge (three shared lemmas collapsing to one) and that bounded Romans 15:19's shared *plēroō* (item 76).** **CONSEQUENCE: if the four DO denote one horizon, then Matthew 28:20's "until" runs to whatever Matthew 24 describes, and the Commission's DURATION and Matthew 24's TIMING become the same question — making item 77 the hinge of BOTH the preterist timeline AND the Great Commission.** **Contexts to examine: 13:39–43 (weeds — harvest, angels, furnace) · 13:47–50 (net — separation) · 24:3 against 24:34's "this generation" · 28:20.** **⚠️ STATED IN ADVANCE: if they do NOT point to one horizon, the preterist hypothesis must absorb that evidence.** **This is Stage 27.**

87. **Does the recursive-commission argument establish individual obligation or only communal continuation? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01f-matthew-28-the-great-commission-in-context]] §8–§10)** **The argument at full strength:** Yeshua commanded the eleven to make disciples, then to teach those disciples *"all that I have commanded you"* — therefore disciple-making is itself taught, therefore reproduces indefinitely. **NOT DISMISSED — probably the strongest textual case for the Commission being structurally ongoing.** **❗ BUT IT CANNOT DISTRIBUTE EVERY COMMAND IDENTICALLY: Yeshua also commanded the apostles things specifically apostolic or situational — Matthew 10's mission instructions, themselves restricted to Israel, are the example. If "everything I commanded" distributed uniformly, every believer would have to be apostle, prophet, teacher AND evangelist, which 1 Corinthians 12:29 denies.** **ESTABLISHES MORE READILY: the community continues making disciples. LESS READILY: every individual member carries an identical personal commission to proclaim throughout the earth.** **Requires closing item 66's retrieval debt (Eph 4:11, 1 Cor 12:29) before the denial can be cited as verified.**

89. **🔑 Does Daniel 12 describe ONE resurrection event or can it accommodate a STAGED pattern? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01g-what-age-is-ending-in-matthew]] §10 and [[01h-who-is-alive-during-and-after-the-thousand-years]] §4)** **Daniel 12:1–3 VERIFIED: Michael arises · "a time of trouble, such as never has been since there was a nation" · "**many** of those who sleep in the dust of the earth shall awake, some to everlasting life, and some to shame and everlasting contempt" · "those who are wise shall **shine like the brightness of the sky**" · 12:4 "seal the book, **until the time of the end**" · 12:13 "you shall rest and shall stand in your allotted place **at the end of the days**."** **🔑 HEBREW VERIFIED: Daniel 12:2 reads וְרַבִּים — *rabbim*, "MANY," NOT "all."** **Set against Revelation 20:5's staged pattern — first resurrection → thousand years → "the rest of the dead."** **❗ MATTHEW 13:43's "the righteous will shine like the sun" APPEARS TO ECHO DANIEL 12:3, which drags Daniel into Matthew's age-consummation question whether or not this project wants it there.** **THIS MAY BECOME THE HARDEST TEST OF THE ENTIRE MODEL: if Matthew's age-ending harvest echoes Daniel 12, what does Daniel's resurrection language mean and where does Daniel locate it?** **⚠️ *rabbim* was retrieved and then DELIBERATELY SET DOWN — building on it now would be the premature synchronization the method forbids. Carried into Stage 30 as the pressure test.**

90. **🔑 The overlapping-ages hypothesis — did the apostolic period sit at a TRANSITION between ages? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01g-what-age-is-ending-in-matthew]] §14–§15)** **Matthew 12:32 VERIFIED: "either in **this age** or in **the age to come**" — MATTHEW HIMSELF RECOGNIZES AT LEAST TWO AGES. That is textual, not inferred.** **HYPOTHESIS (Tier D): Yeshua and the apostles lived during a transition in which one age was ending while another was arriving.** **It would explain how the New Covenant can already operate WHILE THE TEMPLE STILL STANDS**, and why Hebrews 8:13 uses **process** language rather than pretending the first covenant never existed. **Fits the pattern found repeatedly since Stage 14: TRANSITION rather than instantaneous category replacement.** **The AD 30–70 window would then be roughly a generation-long covenantal overlap — and Yeshua repeatedly says "this generation."** **🚧 HISTORICALLY COHERENT ≠ TEXTUALLY DEMONSTRATED. Whether MATTHEW explicitly constructs the transition this way still needs proving.** **This is Stage 28.**

91. **What is the date of Hebrews, and how much does the argument depend on it? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01g-what-age-is-ending-in-matthew]] §15)** **Hebrews 8:13 VERIFIED: "In speaking of a new covenant, he makes the first one obsolete. And what is **becoming obsolete and growing old is ready to vanish away**."** **The temporal language — becoming · growing old · ready to vanish — fits a transitional covenantal period extraordinarily well IF Hebrews predates the Temple's destruction.** **❗ THE DATE OF HEBREWS IS DISPUTED.** **CLASSIFICATION: POTENTIAL CORROBORATION, DEPENDENT ON DATING.** **This project may not use Hebrews 8:13 as evidence for a pre-AD-70 transition without addressing the dating question directly — and must state plainly how much of the argument would survive a later date.**

94. **🔑 Does Revelation proceed chronologically, by recapitulation, or by some combination? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01h-who-is-alive-during-and-after-the-thousand-years]] §6)** **LIVE POSITIONS: recapitulation** (multiple visionary cycles describing overlapping events from different perspectives) · **predominantly sequential progression** · **combinations.** **❗ REVELATION'S LITERARY STRUCTURE MUST BE ESTABLISHED BEFORE ANY seals → trumpets → bowls → millennium SEQUENCE IS USED, and before any mapping onto external history.** **This is its own substantial investigation. Assuming a strict chronological log because it reads like one would be exactly the kind of unexamined premise this project exists to catch.**

95. **🔑 WHAT WOULD FALSIFY THE LITTLE-SEASON HYPOTHESIS? (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01h-who-is-alive-during-and-after-the-thousand-years]] §9)** **TO BE STATED IN ADVANCE OF INTERPRETING REVELATION 20 — not afterward.** **This project began from a preterist-leaning intuition (Revelation 20's millennium as already fulfilled, the present as the "little season") and has spent 27 stages keeping that hypothesis DOWNSTREAM of the evidence (Thesis Discipline, Stage 21½).** **❗ Stating the falsification condition before reaching the text is what would distinguish this project from starting with little-season preterism and constructing an argument for it. Point 12 of the evidence structure, applied to the project's own founding intuition.** **Must be written BEFORE Revelation 20 is opened.**

96. **🔑 Hebrews 2:5's *oikoumenēn tēn mellousan* — the same word as Matthew 24:14, attached to a COMING order. (raised 2026-08-16, see [[01i-this-age-and-the-age-to-come]] §9)** **GREEK VERIFIED BY INTERLINEAR: οἰκουμένην (Strong's 3625) — NOT *kosmos*, NOT *aiōn*. "The inhabited world TO COME."** **❗❗ THIS CUTS AGAINST A LAZY PRETERIST USE OF THE TERM.** Stage 25 established, via Luke 2:1 and Acts 11:28, that *oikoumenē* need not mean every geographical location on the earth — **Hebrews now demonstrates the same word can denote a FUTURE order.** **NEITHER OBSERVATION CANCELS THE OTHER: together they establish that the referent must be determined PER OCCURRENCE, which is the Universal-Language Scope Test operating against this project rather than for it.** **Any argument that leaned on "*oikoumenē* = the first-century imperial inhabited world" must now account for Hebrews 2:5.** **Hebrews connects it with Psalm 8 — humanity crowned with glory and honour, all things subjected beneath his feet, centred in Yeshua. ⚠️ Psalm 8 and Hebrews 2:6–9 NOT retrieved (item 97).** **Feeds q68's comparative scope study, which must now include this occurrence.**

98. **🔑🔑 THE FIRST-RESURRECTION PROBLEM — four possibilities, all kept live. (raised 2026-08-17, see [[01j-resurrection-before-revelation]] §12)** **A hypothesis that the millennium already occurred survives ONLY if the FIRST RESURRECTION preceding it can be explained.** **(1)** A literal bodily resurrection of the martyrs already occurred · **(2)** "first resurrection" means something other than bodily resurrection **and Revelation itself supplies sufficient evidence for that** · **(3)** the millennium's chronology is not where this project currently assumes it is · **(4) THE BROADER HYPOTHESIS IS WRONG.** **❗❗ THIS PROJECT MUST BE WILLING TO LAND ON #4. That is the falsifiability requirement, stated BEFORE Revelation is read rather than after.** **AND THE BURDEN FALLS ON THE PREFERRED READING FIRST: because bodily resurrection is strongly established at Daniel 12, John 5:28–29, Luke 20:34–36, 1 Corinthians 15:42–54 and 1 Thessalonians 4:16, a SYMBOLIC first-resurrection reading of Revelation 20 carries a burden of proof.** **Pairs with item 95 — the falsification condition still to be written before Revelation 20 is opened.**

(raised 2026-08-17, see [[01j-resurrection-before-revelation]] §10)** **GREEK VERIFIED BY INTERLINEAR: ἀπάντησιν — *apantēsin*, Strong's 529, "a meeting" (from *apantaō*, to encounter).** **Some argue the term can evoke citizens going out to meet an arriving dignitary AND ESCORTING HIM BACK IN. Others caution against building a rigid technical rule from a few usages.** **🔖 BOOKMARKED, NOT SETTLED.** **May matter later for whether believers are taken AWAY permanently or go out to MEET a coming king — which would bear on the shape of any "coming" chronology.** **⚠️ The resurrection itself remains explicit regardless of how *apantēsis* resolves — this question does not touch item 98.**

SIMILARITY → QUESTION, NOT IDENTITY.** **Same brake that killed the Stage 23 shared-lemma bridge and bounded Stage 26's four *synteleia* occurrences.** **Any chronology that silently merges these trumpets has assumed its conclusion.**

102. **🔑 ZECHARIAH 14:16–19 — a formal millennial control text. (raised 2026-08-17, see [[01k-can-an-age-end-while-resurrection-remains-ahead]] §10)** **ESV VERIFIED: "everyone who survives of all the nations that have come against Jerusalem shall go up **year after year** to worship the King, the LORD of hosts, and **to keep the Feast of Booths**. And if any of the families of the earth do not go up… **there will be no rain on them**"** — with **Egypt named specifically** and a plague attached. **DETAILS CORRECTED against a remembered version: it is SUKKOT, not Passover; YEAR AFTER YEAR, not twice yearly.** **✅ ESTABLISHED: Zechariah anticipates a period in which surviving nations worship Yahweh, with Sukkot, Jerusalem and rain connected. ❌ NOT ESTABLISHED: that this scene is IDENTICAL to Revelation 20's millennium — Zechariah never says "during the thousand years."** **→ A MAJOR FUTURE CONTROL TEXT for the millennial hypothesis, but NOT YET A DEFEATER OF IT.** **❗ AND THE CIRCULARITY THE METHOD CATCHES: using Zechariah 14 as evidence that the millennium has not happened REQUIRES first establishing that it describes the millennium — otherwise: "this hasn't happened because the millennium hasn't; I know the millennium hasn't because this hasn't."** **The objection is genuine once its missing step — the ZECHARIAH 14 → REVELATION 20 IDENTITY — is supplied. Preserved rather than dismissed.**

103. **Are Revelation 6's martyrs the same group as Revelation 20's first-resurrection rulers? (raised 2026-08-17, see [[01k-can-an-age-end-while-resurrection-remains-ahead]] §11)** **Revelation 6:9–11 ESV VERIFIED: "the souls of those who had been slain for the word of God and for the witness they had borne… told to rest a little longer, **until the number of their fellow servants and their brothers should be complete**, who were to be killed as they themselves had been."** **Revelation 20:4 describes those "beheaded for the testimony of Jesus and for the word of God" who "came to life and reigned."** **AN INTERTEXTUAL QUESTION INSIDE REVELATION ITSELF — martyrs waiting for a full number, then martyrs reigning.** **CANNOT be answered before Revelation is read sequentially from chapter 1.** **Bears directly on item 98's first-resurrection problem.**

104. **🔑 THREE CONTESTED VERSES, THREE UNDETERMINED VOICES — is the recurrence itself significant? (raised 2026-08-17, see [[01l-one-taken-and-one-left]] §4)** **VERIFIED BY INTERLINEAR, INDEPENDENTLY, THREE TIMES: Romans 9:22's κατηρτισμένα (perfect participle, middle or passive — Stage 22) · Acts 13:48's τεταγμένοι (perfect participle, middle or passive — Stage 25½) · Matthew 24:40's παραλαμβάνεται and ἀφίεται (present indicative, middle or passive — Stage 30½).** **❗ THREE OF THE MOST CONTESTED ESCHATOLOGICAL/SOTERIOLOGICAL VERSES IN THE CANON REST ON GRAMMATICALLY UNDETERMINED VOICE, AND IN NONE OF THEM DOES THE GRAMMAR SUPPLY AN AGENT.** **NO INTERPRETIVE CAMP CAN CLAIM THE GRAMMAR IN ANY OF THEM.** **Whether this recurrence is coincidence, a feature of the Greek verbal system, or something the texts are doing deliberately, is NOT ESTABLISHED — but the pattern is now documented across three independent retrievals and should not be rediscovered a fourth time by accident.**

105. **🔑 Is Matthew 24:40–41 / Luke 17:34–37 the same event as 1 Corinthians 15:51–52 / 1 Thessalonians 4:16–17? (raised 2026-08-17, see [[01l-one-taken-and-one-left]] §2–§5)** **❗ THE NOAH/LOT CONTEXT AND LUKE'S "WHERE?" MUST BE RESOLVED BEFORE IDENTITY IS ASSERTED.** **Matthew's own analogy has the flood "sweep away" the WICKED immediately before "one will be taken."** **Luke 17:37 VERIFIED: the disciples ask "Where, Lord?" and Yeshua answers "**Where the corpse is, there the vultures will gather**" — which sounds like judgment, not transportation of saints to a protected kingdom.** **TWO SEPARATIONS CURRENTLY IN DANGER OF BEING COLLAPSED: (a) a possible JUDGMENT separation (Matthew 24 / Luke 17); (b) RESURRECTION / TRANSFORMATION / GATHERING (1 Cor 15 / 1 Thess 4).** **If Scripture establishes they are the same event, excellent. If it establishes they are not, the hypothesis must accommodate that.** **SIMILARITY CREATES THE INVESTIGATION; IT DOES NOT ESTABLISH IDENTITY.** **Sub-question: does Paul's instantaneous transformation entail GEOGRAPHIC RELOCATION? Paul says the living are "changed" and "caught up… to meet the Lord" — he does not say they were moved to another location on the earth.** **Sub-question: are Paul's gathered saints IDENTICAL with Revelation 20's reigning saints (who are described as beheaded for the testimony and as not having worshipped the beast)?**

106. **Do Matthew 24, Mark 13 and Luke 21 compress one horizon or contain distinguishable stages? Surgical work, bookmarked before Revelation.**

108. **What is Satan actually prevented from doing during the thousand years? (raised 2026-08-17, see [[01l-one-taken-and-one-left]] §8)** **Revelation 20:3 gives a SPECIFIED restraint with a STATED PURPOSE: "so that he might not deceive the nations any longer."** **TWO EXPANSIONS REFUSED: (a) that Satan exercised zero influence of any kind anywhere; (b) that the millennium was therefore sinless.** **Genesis 6:5, Mark 7:21–23 and James 1:14 locate evil within the human heart independently of external deception, so A BOUND DECEIVER DOES NOT ENTAIL A SINLESS CIVILIZATION** — **which defeats the common objection "there was warfare and corruption between AD 300 and AD 1300, therefore Satan cannot have been bound," since that argument ASSUMES Revelation defines the binding as the elimination of human sin. IT DOES NOT.** **REMAINING QUESTIONS: what precisely does "deceiving the nations" MEAN? And does the binding restrict only Satan's DIRECT activity, or also whatever agents ordinarily participate in that deception?** **Neither a loophole nor a total cessation may be invented before Revelation speaks. Saved for Revelation 20.**

109. **🔑 FALSIFICATION TEST — if the gathering occurred historically, what observable trace should it have left? (raised 2026-08-17, see [[01l-one-taken-and-one-left]] §14)** **❗ TO BE RUN AS A FALSIFICATION TEST, NOT A CONFIRMATION SEARCH.** **NOT: "find evidence for the little season." INSTEAD: "do first- or early-second-century Jewish, Roman, Christian or other surviving sources describe an unexplained disappearance of a recognizable population corresponding to those the hypothesis says were gathered?"** **Absence would NOT automatically disprove the hypothesis — ancient documentation is fragmentary — but it would INCREASE THE EXPLANATORY BURDEN. Credible independent sources describing something relevant would need investigation on their own terms.** **⚠️ Depends on item 105 first: WHO was gathered must be established before WHERE THEY WENT or WHAT TRACE REMAINED can be asked.** **Governed by the Report–Event Distinction and the Compatibility–Evidence Distinction, and by the research design at [[01l-one-taken-and-one-left]] §9: FREEZE THE BIBLICAL PREDICTION FIRST, THEN INSPECT THE HISTORICAL DATASET.**

110. **⚠️ CORRECTION — Daniel 12:2's *rabbim* does NOT establish "not all." (raised 2026-08-18, see [[01m-daniel-12-resurrection-sealing-and-the-time-of-the-end]] §3; supersedes the narrower framing carried since Stage 27)** **Biblical "many" can function as a BROAD COLLECTIVE rather than mathematically meaning "some but definitely not all."** **So the inference — *"Daniel says many, therefore perhaps only Revelation 20's first-resurrection martyrs rise"* — IS TOO QUICK. Possible; NOT established by the word alone.** **❗ THIS WOULD HAVE BEEN AN OVERREACH IN THE HYPOTHESIS'S FAVOUR, which is why it is formally blocked rather than quietly retired.** **Daniel 12:2 leaves the SCOPE question more open than an English arithmetic reading suggests.**

111. **Daniel 12:4 — what are "running to and fro" and "knowledge"? (raised 2026-08-18, see [[01m-daniel-12-resurrection-sealing-and-the-time-of-the-end]] §6)** **Hebrew VERIFIED: יְשֹׁטְטוּ *yeshoteṭu* (Revised JPS: "range far and wide") · הַדָּעַת *ha-daʿat* — WITH THE DEFINITE ARTICLE.** **❗ EXPLICITLY REJECTED: Daniel 12:4 = cars, airplanes, the internet — unless lexical and contextual evidence earns it.** **The verb may involve ranging about or searching THROUGH THE BOOK; "the knowledge" may concern understanding THE PROPHECY. Note the position: immediately after the sealing command and immediately before Daniel says he did not understand.** Textbook Anachronism Test.

112. **Daniel 12:7 — what is "the shattering of the power of the holy people"? (raised 2026-08-18, see [[01m-daniel-12-resurrection-sealing-and-the-time-of-the-end]] §7)** **THE COMPLETION MARKER IS AN EVENT, NOT A CALENDAR DATE: "when the shattering of the power of the holy people comes to an end all these things would be finished."** **"The holy people" in Daniel's context is DANIEL'S PEOPLE — placing the prophecy inside Israel's covenantal story.** **Potentially highly relevant to first-century Jerusalem/Temple chronology — ⚠️ PRECISION REQUIRED BEFORE IDENTIFYING IT WITH AD 70.** Also: "time, times, and half a time" recurs at Daniel 7:25 and is later associated with 42 months / 1,260 days — **🚧 we may NOT assume every 3½-type period across Daniel and Revelation is the same historical interval.**

113. **Daniel 12:11–12 — the 1,290 and 1,335 days. (raised 2026-08-18, see [[01m-daniel-12-resurrection-sealing-and-the-time-of-the-end]] §10–§11)** **The prophecy is anchored to an explicitly TEMPLE/SACRIFICIAL marker: "from the time that the regular burnt offering is taken away and the abomination that makes desolate is set up."** **Difference between the two figures: 45 days.** **❗ NO NUMEROLOGY FISHING. Required order BEFORE any historical candidate is consulted: (1) identify the starting marker · (2) establish the calendar assumptions · (3) determine whether the days are literal or symbolic · (4) ONLY THEN compare historical candidates.** Governed by the same research design as the millennium: **freeze the prediction, then inspect the dataset.**

115. **Revelation 1:10 — what is "the Lord's day"? (raised 2026-08-18, see [[01n-revelation-1-the-book-tells-us-how-to-read-it]])** **Greek: ἐν τῇ κυριακῇ ἡμέρᾳ.** **THREE LIVE READINGS: Sunday · Sabbath · prophetic transport into the Day of Yahweh.** **❗ DO NOT DECIDE FROM LATER CHURCH TERMINOLOGY — and NONE of the three may be allowed to control Revelation's chronology before being established.**

116. **Revelation 1:6 / 5:10 — is Revelation 20 introducing a NEW priestly status, or consummating one Revelation already attributes to believers? (raised 2026-08-18)** **1:6 "made us a kingdom, priests to his God and Father" · 5:10 "a kingdom and priests… they shall reign on the earth" — BOTH BEFORE Revelation 20 exists in the narrative.** Reconnects the Melchizedek/priest-king branch. **Feeds q129.**

117. **Revelation 1:7 — "those who pierced him." (raised 2026-08-18, see [[01n-revelation-1-the-book-tells-us-how-to-read-it]])** **John does not merely say someday people will mourn; he specifies those who pierced Him.** Historically the crucifixion involved first-century actors, and Acts can address Jerusalem audiences corporately as "whom you crucified." **⚠️ Representative/corporate language complicates simplistic biological identification — BUT THE HISTORICAL ANCHOR IS REAL, and it fits Matthew 24's generational horizon.** The verse fuses **Daniel 7** with **Zechariah 12** — the "tribes of the land" work paying off.

118. **Does Revelation 1:19 supply the book's outline? (raised 2026-08-18)** "the things that you have seen, those that are, and those that are to take place after this." **⚠️ MAY have value — must NOT be assumed to give a rigid chapter-by-chapter chronology**, especially since apocalyptic can revisit · recapitulate · zoom · repeat from different perspectives. **Sequential vs recapitulative vs combination must be DISCOVERED (q94).**

119. **Revelation 12:4's stars — angelic beings? (raised 2026-08-18)** **Revelation 1:20 demonstrates John CAN use stars for angelic beings — which makes the angelic reading of 12:4 SUBSTANTIALLY MORE PLAUSIBLE. ⚠️ PLAUSIBLE ≠ ESTABLISHED: symbols can change referents by context.** When Revelation 12 is reached, compare stars · dragon · war in heaven · Michael · casting down against Daniel · Job · Isaiah · "I saw Satan fall" · the cross/resurrection · Revelation's internal chronology.

121. **Did Messiah descend to the realm of the dead between death and resurrection — and what occurred there? (raised 2026-08-18, see [[01o-jonah-sheol-three-days-and-the-keys-of-death]])** **Legitimate intertextual question, UNSOLVED.** **Psalm 16 / Acts 2** — "you will not abandon my soul to Hades" (explicit Messiah–Hades connection, stronger than inferring from the keys) · **Ephesians 4:9** — "descended into the lower regions," ⚠️ genuine grammatical question whether that means *the earth* or *the underworld* · **1 Peter 3:18–20** — "the spirits in prison" · **1 Peter 4:6** — "the gospel was preached even to the dead." **And the "did He bring people out?" question has real material: Matthew 27:52–53 (tombs opened, many bodies of the saints raised) and Ephesians 4:8 ("he led a host of captives") — ⚠️ HISTORICAL THEOLOGICAL INTERPRETATION ≠ EXPLICIT DESCRIPTION. Investigate; do NOT write into the chronology.**

122. **⚠️ The three-compartment Sheol scheme needs verification before use. (raised 2026-08-18)** **1 Enoch 22 does contain a developed geography of the realm of the dead with separated hollow places differentiated by righteousness/wickedness/judgment — but the precise three-part scheme as recalled (never knew Yahweh / knew and rejected / knew and accepted) MUST BE VERIFIED against the actual textual tradition before being crystallized.** **STANDING RULE REAFFIRMED: Enoch may illuminate Second Temple conceptual vocabulary; it may NOT define Tanakh or NT passages before those are exegeted independently.** **Required order: Tanakh/Sheol → Yeshua and NT → Second Temple literature → later interpretation.** **And Luke 16's rich man and Lazarus will be unavoidable — compare INDEPENDENTLY first.**

123. **Is Proverbs 8's personified Wisdom directly Yeshua? (raised 2026-08-18)** **Proverbs presents ḥokmah — Wisdom, grammatically feminine and poetically personified, present before the depths and during creation.** **A potentially profound typological/Christological reading — ⚠️ it may NOT be converted into "Proverbs explicitly says the speaker is Yeshua." IT DOESN'T.** **Governed by CLEAR-TEXT LOAD BEARING: Messiah's pre-existence rests on John 1, Colossians 1, John 17 — so Proverbs 8 can remain a fascinating question without carrying weight it cannot bear.**

124. **⚠️ "Elohim" needs a brake before use. (raised 2026-08-18)** ***Elohim* is a Hebrew grammatical/semantic category whose referent varies by context** — most famously Yahweh, but usable in other contexts involving heavenly beings. **Saying "Yeshua is part of the Elohim" could accidentally imply a pantheon of equivalent gods, which is not the intent.** **CLEANER QUESTION: how does Scripture describe the relationship between Yahweh, the pre-existent Messiah/Word, the Spirit, and the heavenly council?** **A massive future branch. Do NOT let English "God/gods" baggage settle the Hebrew question before *el*, *elohim*, *bene elohim* are investigated.**

125. **⚠️ RETRIEVAL DEBT — Stage 33.** **CLOSED: Revelation 2:5 · 2:16 · 3:3 · 3:20–22 · 3:10 Greek.** **STILL OPEN: Revelation 2:10 · 2:11 · 2:20–27 · 3:8–9 · 3:11–12 verbatim · Acts 17:6 · Acts 19:27 · Psalm 2 · Psalm 110.**

127. **Revelation 3:9's "synagogue of Satan" — historical particularity must be preserved. (raised 2026-08-18)** **⚠️ THIS TEXT HAS BEEN WEAPONIZED HISTORICALLY AGAINST JEWISH PEOPLE. That violates both context and this project's methodology.** Yeshua addresses **a specific first-century conflict** involving people "who say that they are Jews and are not, but lie." **Whatever that designation means, it CANNOT responsibly become "Jews generally = synagogue of Satan." THAT IS NOT WHAT THE TEXT SAYS.** **HISTORICAL CONFLICT REMAINS HISTORICALLY PARTICULAR UNTIL EVIDENCE EXPANDS IT.** *(Consistent with Stage 21½'s "ancient Israel ≠ the modern State.")*

(raised 2026-08-18, see [[01q-forbidden-to-speak-the-word-in-asia]]; refines q81)** **ESTABLISHED: the Spirit prevented that missionary movement into Asia · God redirected them toward Macedonia · Asia SUBSEQUENTLY OPENED · Ephesus became a major centre of proclamation (Acts 19:8–10, VERIFIED).** **❌ UNKNOWN: precisely WHY Yah wanted Macedonia first.** **⚠️ AND A HYPOTHESIS CORRECTED: the suggestion that the prohibition was because those places "already knew the word" is NOT what Acts says — and substantial evangelistic work remained when Paul reached Ephesus.** Governed by the Providence–Inference Boundary.

129. **🔑🔑 CENTRAL REGISTER — WHAT DISTINGUISHES REVELATION 20'S THOUSAND-YEAR REIGN FROM THE REIGN AND KINGDOM REVELATION SAYS ALREADY EXISTED WHEN JOHN WROTE? (raised 2026-08-18, see [[01p-revelation-2-3-seven-assemblies-repeated-comings-and-the-coming-trial]] §17, §21)** **VERIFIED: Revelation 3:21 — "as I also CONQUERED AND SAT DOWN with my Father on his throne." PAST TENSE.** Plus **1:5** ruler of kings on earth · **1:6** made us a kingdom and priests · **1:9** John already a partner **in the kingdom** · **5:10** kingdom and priests who shall reign.** **❗❗ THEREFORE "KINGDOM LANGUAGE = MILLENNIUM" IS DEMONSTRABLY TOO SIMPLE, and "reign appears → therefore the millennium begins here" IS NO LONGER AVAILABLE.** **Candidate answers, NONE selected: a particular PHASE of the reign · a different SET of participants · recapitulation · something else.** **THIS IS A PRIMARY FALSIFIER OF THE POST-MILLENNIAL LITTLE-SEASON HYPOTHESIS — it must be answered FROM REVELATION, and the hypothesis became more falsifiable the moment it was posed.**

(raised 2026-08-18, see [[01r-revelation-4-5-the-throne-the-scroll-and-the-lamb]])** **Revelation 5:5–6 establishes that John can HEAR one identification and SEE another image describing THE SAME REFERENT.** **🔖 FLAGGED AND DELIBERATELY NOT PURSUED — but if Revelation does this again with a numbered/sealed group and a great multitude, the relationship between what John hears and what he sees could be decisive.** **Do not jump there. Let Revelation reach it.**

Flag; do not settle.** **Also feeds the REVELATION 5 EXALTATION HYPOTHESIS, since 12:5 is a second throne-ascent image.**

(raised 2026-08-18, see [[01s-the-dragons-tail-son-of-perdition-and-the-prodigal]])** **12:4 VERIFIED: "His tail swept down a third of the stars of heaven and cast them to the earth" — the DRAGON HIMSELF casts them down.** **12:7–9 VERIFIED: an explicit war — Michael and his angels against the dragon and his angels; "he was defeated"; "the great dragon was thrown down… and his angels were thrown down with him."** **⚠️ FORMULATION CORRECTED: it is NOT "Satan lost and therefore had to escort the others out." THE TEXT DOES NOT SAY THAT.** **Possibilities, none selected: different events · one describes participation in the rebellion and the other its consequence · overlapping imagery.**

134. **🔑 Is Revelation 12:9's "deceiver of the whole world" *oikoumenē* (3625)? Retrieve before Revelation 12.**

⚠️ **CORRECTION RECORDED — SCRIPTURE DOES NOT CALL SATAN "THE SON OF PERDITION."** Greek ὁ υἱὸς τῆς ἀπωλείας occurs at **John 17:12 (JUDAS)** and **2 Thessalonians 2:3 (the man of lawlessness)** — both VERIFIED. Whether the latter figure bears some relationship to Satan is a **separate interpretive question.** And "son of X" is Semitic idiom for *characterized by / destined for*, not offspring. *(See [[01s-the-dragons-tail-son-of-perdition-and-the-prodigal]] §2.)*

(raised 2026-08-18, see [[01t-revelation-6-the-seals-the-martyrs-and-cosmic-collapse]] §2)** **VERIFIED: white horse · a bow · a crown GIVEN to him · came out conquering and to conquer.** **Proposed: Yeshua · the gospel · military conquest · a counterfeit messiah · imperial conquest · others.** **❗ YESHUA IS TEMPTING BECAUSE REVELATION 19 LATER HAS HIM ON A WHITE HORSE — but SAME SYMBOL ≠ NECESSARILY SAME REFERENT, and Revelation 19 explicitly identifies its rider in ways Revelation 6 does not.** **DESCRIPTION BEFORE IDENTIFICATION. Left open.**

Revelation 6:8 lists the same four.** **⚠️ THIS ESTABLISHES VOCABULARY ANCESTRY, NOT TARGET IDENTIFICATION — Ezekiel's oracle had its own historical referent.** **AND THE CLUSTER: Matthew 23 (righteous blood · the altar · this generation · Jerusalem) · Matthew 24 (temple · cosmic signs · this generation) · Luke 23:28–31 VERIFIED (Yeshua quotes Hosea 10:8's mountains-fall-on-us and directs it at Jerusalem's women and children) · Revelation 6:16 (same imagery).** **🆕 AND THE ALTAR APPEARS ON BOTH SIDES: Matthew 23:35 locates a murder BETWEEN THE SANCTUARY AND THE ALTAR; Revelation 6:9 locates the martyrs UNDER THE ALTAR.** **THE CLUSTER CANNOT RESPONSIBLY BE IGNORED — AND DOES NOT YET PROVE IDENTITY.**

(raised 2026-08-18, see [[01t-revelation-6-the-seals-the-martyrs-and-cosmic-collapse]] §15)** **VERIFIED: the stars of the sky fell to the earth as the fig tree sheds its winter fruit when shaken by a gale.** **❗ REVELATION 6 ITSELF DOES NOT IDENTIFY THESE STARS AS ANGELS — contrast Revelation 1:20, where the seven stars ARE explicitly the angels of the assemblies.** **INTERNAL-INTERPRETATION PRIORITY CUTS BOTH WAYS: it licenses a definition WHERE GIVEN and FORBIDS importing it where it is NOT.** **LEAVE THE REFERENT OPEN. More evidence when Revelation 12 gives dragon + stars + angels together (q119, q132).**

(raised 2026-08-18, see [[01t-revelation-6-the-seals-the-martyrs-and-cosmic-collapse]] §19)** **Undetermined, and deliberately so.** **Interacts with q94 (is Revelation chronological, recapitulative, or both?) and with the VISION SEQUENCE ≠ EVENT SEQUENCE rule.** **⚠️ The REVELATION 5 EXALTATION HYPOTHESIS survived Revelation 6 — its falsification condition was that the seals would require events demonstrably beyond the post-exaltation horizon, and they did not. THAT IS SURVIVAL, NOT PROOF.**

141. **Matthew 24:23–27's localized-Messiah warning — what does it establish, and what does it not? (raised 2026-08-18, see [[01u-if-they-say-he-is-in-the-wilderness]] Part Two)** **VERIFIED: "if they say to you, 'Look, he is in the wilderness,' do not go out. If they say, 'Look, he is in the inner rooms,' do not believe it. For as the lightning comes from the east and shines as far as the west, so will be the coming of the Son of Man."** **✅ ESTABLISHES: His followers were not to identify the *parousia* by someone supplying Messiah's physical coordinates; PHYSICAL-LOCATION CLAIMS ARE NOT RELIABLE EVIDENCE OF HIS PROMISED COMING.** **❌ DOES NOT ESTABLISH: the location or mode of the millennial reign · that Messiah would be physically hidden somewhere · Rupes Nigra · that the *parousia* was invisible.** **⚠️ THE INFERENCE "therefore the millennial reign occurred somewhere physically inaccessible" IS NOT ESTABLISHED BY THIS TEXT — and it arrived immediately after a genuine observation, which is exactly when an unearned extension is hardest to notice.**

142. **🆕 Matthew 24:28 and Luke 17:37 are the SAME SAYING in different contexts. (raised 2026-08-18, see [[01u-if-they-say-he-is-in-the-wilderness]] §10; feeds item 105)** **Matthew 24:28 VERIFIED: "Wherever the corpse is, there the vultures will gather" — placed immediately AFTER the lightning/*parousia* statement.** **Luke 17:37 VERIFIED (Side Branch 30½): the same line is Yeshua's ANSWER to the disciples' question "Where, Lord?" after the one-taken-one-left sayings.** **❗ THE SAME SAYING SITS IN BOTH CONTEXTS — a real datum for whether the taken/left sayings describe the same event as Paul's gathering (item 105), and it was NOT visible until Matthew 24:23–28 was retrieved as a BLOCK rather than stopping at the lightning verse.** **⚠️ Greek not retrieved for either (*sōma* vs *ptōma*) — item 144.**

143. **🔑 Does Revelation 20 specify the GEOGRAPHICAL LOCATION of the thousand-year reign at all? (raised 2026-08-18, see [[01u-if-they-say-he-is-in-the-wilderness]] §15–§16)** **PRIOR QUESTIONS THAT MUST COME FIRST, before "where was the millennium?": what does Revelation 20 actually say HAPPENS during the thousand years · WHO participates · are participants described bodily, spiritually, resurrected, transformed, heavenly, earthly, or some combination · what does "reigned with Messiah" MEAN there · and only then, does the text give a location.** **❗ IF REVELATION DOESN'T SAY WHERE, WE CANNOT FILL THAT BLANK MERELY BECAUSE A PROPOSED GEOGRAPHY WOULD SOLVE THE PROBLEM.** **AND "INACCESSIBLE" IS AT LEAST SIX DISTINCT POSSIBILITIES, NOT ONE: spatial separation · heavenly/earthly separation · spiritual visibility/invisibility · transformed bodily condition · symbolic visionary representation · ordinary geographical separation · or something unanticipated.** **Rupes Nigra stays disciplined: ancient maps could become evidence for WHAT PEOPLE BELIEVED about geography; they cannot supply a location Revelation never identifies (see [[07-deferred-historical-register]] H9, H12).** **⚖️ And what keeps the hypothesis interesting: Revelation 20:8 has Satan deceiving "the nations at the four corners of the earth" — NATIONS APPARENTLY EXIST TO BE DECEIVED, so the relationship between reigning saints and ordinary populations is a question REVELATION ITSELF FORCES (q93).**

145. **Ezekiel 9's mark and Revelation 7's seal — pattern established, target not. (raised 2026-08-18, see [[01v-revelation-7-the-144000-and-the-innumerable-multitude]] §2)** **Ezekiel 9:3-4 VERIFIED: "Pass through the city, THROUGH JERUSALEM, and put a mark on the FOREHEADS of the men who sigh and groan over all the abominations that are committed in it."** **Revelation 7:3 VERIFIED: "Do not harm the earth or the sea or the trees, UNTIL we have sealed the servants of our God on their FOREHEADS."** **Both: judgment restrained → faithful identified → mark on foreheads → judgment proceeds.** **A VERY STRONG INTERTEXT, and it cannot be ignored given the first-century correspondences accumulating from Revelation 6.** **⚠️ AND THE BRAKE: Ezekiel's oracle had its own historical referent. This establishes the PATTERN, not Revelation 7's target.** **AND NOTE: "sealed" does NOT mean physically removed — Ezekiel's marked righteous REMAIN in the place facing judgment, distinguished from those under condemnation. DO NOT import a removal mechanism here.**

146. **Why is the Revelation 7 tribal list arranged as it is? (raised 2026-08-18, see [[01v-revelation-7-the-144000-and-the-innumerable-multitude]] §7-§8)** **VERIFIED IN FULL, 12,000 from each: Judah · Reuben · Gad · Asher · Naphtali · Manasseh · Simeon · Levi · Issachar · Zebulun · Joseph · Benjamin.** **OBSERVATIONS: JUDAH FIRST despite Reuben being Jacob's firstborn · LEVI included though often omitted from territorial enumerations · MANASSEH included though he is JOSEPH'S SON, not Jacob's · EPHRAIM not named · DAN ABSENT though Dan IS one of Jacob's sons.** **⚠️ CORRECTION RECORDED: the list is NOT simply "the original tribes."** **INFERENCE: the list has been INTENTIONALLY ARRANGED — which makes it far less likely John copied an ordinary census without theological intention.** **❌ UNKNOWN: the precise theological reason for every modification. Historical interpretations exist (Dan and Ephraim associated with idolatry; Dan associated in later traditions with evil), BUT REVELATION DOES NOT SAY WHY. Do not invent the reason.** **Judah first is the one easy piece — Revelation 5:5 has just called Messiah "the Lion of the tribe of JUDAH."**

147. **Is Revelation 7:14's "the great tribulation" the same event as Matthew 24:21's? (raised 2026-08-18, see [[01v-revelation-7-the-144000-and-the-innumerable-multitude]] §14)** **Revelation 7:14 VERIFIED: "These are the ones coming out of THE GREAT TRIBULATION" — τῆς θλίψεως τῆς μεγάλης.** **Matthew 24:21 VERIFIED at Stage 31: θλῖψις μεγάλη, in the context of Judea, flight, Sabbath, the Jerusalem/temple discourse, and "this generation."** **A DIRECT LEXICAL CORRESPONDENCE.** **⚠️ LEXICAL IDENTITY ALONE DOES NOT PROVE EVENT IDENTITY — the standing brake since Stage 23's collapse of three shared lemmas to roughly one.** **But it joins the accumulating cluster: Matthew 23 (righteous blood · the altar · this generation) → Matthew 24 (great tribulation · cosmic signs · gathering · this generation) → Luke 23 (Jerusalem · mountains fall on us) → Revelation 6 (martyrs · a little longer · cosmic signs · mountains fall on us) → Revelation 7 (the great tribulation).** **THE CORRESPONDENCE IS BECOMING SUBSTANTIAL. It is still not identity.**

148. **🔑🔑 CENTRAL — ARE THE 144,000 AND THE INNUMERABLE MULTITUDE IDENTICAL, DISTINCT, OR DISTINCT-BUT-INTEGRALLY-RELATED REPRESENTATIONS OF YAH'S REDEEMED PEOPLE? (raised 2026-08-18, see [[01v-revelation-7-the-144000-and-the-innumerable-multitude]] §11-§12, §23-§28)** **RECORDED VERBATIM AND DELIBERATELY UNRESOLVED.** **✅ ESTABLISHED: John HEARS 144,000, explicitly 12,000 from each of twelve named tribes, described through Israel's tribes in a deliberately unusual list, sealed BEFORE judgment proceeds; John then SEES an innumerable multinational multitude emerging from "the great tribulation" and standing before Yah and the Lamb.** **🟡 STRONG HYPOTHESIS: the hear→see construction MAY parallel Revelation 5's Lion→Lamb correspondence.** **🟡 ALTERNATIVE STILL VIABLE: distinct but related populations. 🟡 THIRD POSSIBILITY: distinct-but-integrally-related representations within one redeemed people — distinction without separation, since this project has repeatedly found binary questions badly framed.** **❌ NOT ESTABLISHED: that they are definitely identical · that 144,000 is strictly literal or strictly symbolic · that the 144,000 are exclusively ethnic Jews in a modern racial or political sense · that Revelation 7 describes a modern evacuation event.** **❗ AND THE LATER-DATA FLAG: Revelation 14:1-5 VERIFIED — Mount Zion · the Father's name on foreheads · a song only they can learn · "have not defiled themselves with women, for they are VIRGINS" · follow the Lamb wherever He goes · redeemed as ἀπαρχή, FIRSTFRUITS · blameless.** **This STRENGTHENS identifying Revelation 7's and 14's 144,000 as the same group, while placing ADDITIONAL PRESSURE on any premature equation with the innumerable multitude — because that equation would require explaining why an entire multinational multitude is described as male virgins.** **⚠️ AND WE MAY NOT SIMPLY ANNOUNCE that virgin means spiritually faithful: Scripture does use sexual-fidelity imagery covenantally, and Revelation will use it heavily with Babylon — BUT DECIDING THAT HERE WOULD BE INTERPRETING THE DIFFICULT TEXT ACCORDING TO WHAT OUR PREFERRED HYPOTHESIS NEEDS.** **AND "FIRSTFRUITS" normally implies a relationship between a FIRST PORTION and SOMETHING FOLLOWING (compare 1 Corinthians 15:20), which weighs against a simple equation — PRESSURE, NOT DESTRUCTION, since symbolic visions can describe one corporate reality under different aspects.** **DO NOT RESOLVE UNTIL REVELATION 14 IS EXEGETED IN SEQUENCE.**

150. **What does *porneia* encompass, and how does it differ from *moicheia*? (raised 2026-08-19, see [[01w-sexual-morality-marriage-and-repentance]] §5-§6 — PRIVATE BRANCH)** **Terms to untangle: μοιχεία *moicheia* (adultery, violation of the marriage bond) · πορνεία *porneia* (sexual immorality, broader and harder) · πόρνη *pornē* (prostitute) · πόρνος *pornos*, plus the Torah's own categories.** **❗ TWO SHORTCUTS BOTH REFUSED: that *porneia* simply means "sex before marriage," AND that anything short of adultery is therefore permitted.** **Context before lexicon — the same failure refused at *gē*, *oikoumenē*, *telos*, *Israel* and *resurrection*.**

151. **What biblically CONSTITUTES marriage? (raised 2026-08-19, see [[01w-sexual-morality-marriage-and-repentance]] §8 — PRIVATE)** **Scripture does not present modern church ceremonies, government licences, rings or contemporary customs as the universal mechanism by which marriage comes into existence — so a genuine study is needed.** **⚠️ BUT "we did not marry in a church" is NOT a strong biblical basis for concluding no marriage existed before Yah.** **This makes the question HARDER, not easier — the easier answer was not permitted in either direction.**

They do NOT answer WHAT CONDUCT BECAME PERMISSIBLE.** **Governing rule, supplied by the author himself before any text was consulted: *someone else's failure toward me does not automatically redefine what obedience requires from me*.** **To examine in sequence: Matthew 5 · Matthew 19 · Mark 10 · Luke 16 · Romans 7 · 1 Corinthians 7 · relevant Torah.** **SEPARATION · DIVORCE · ABANDONMENT · ADULTERY · REMARRIAGE · SEXUAL RELATIONS ARE NOT INTERCHANGEABLE CATEGORIES.**

The NT was written TO baptized communities that still had to be warned, corrected and told to repent — AND grace is not permission to deliberately continue what one has become convinced is sin. BOTH SIDES MUST SURVIVE.** **And on Revelation 14:4: HEBREWS 13:4 VERIFIED constrains it — "let marriage be held in honor among all, and let the marriage bed be undefiled" — so Scripture cannot coherently teach that sex itself contaminates. NOR may "defiled with women" become "women/sex = dirty."** **Interacts with item 148; Stage 36's refusal to close the 144,000 identification now matters personally as well as methodologically.**

172. **🔑 NERO'S PERSECUTION — ATTESTED, BUT ITS DURATION AND EXTENT ARE NOT. (raised 2026-08-19, see [[02c-nero-the-apostles-and-the-asian-assemblies]] Q1)** **✅ RETRIEVED: Tacitus *Annals* 15.44 (English translation only, NOT the Latin) — Nero blamed Christians for the fire and inflicted "the most exquisite tortures"; "Christus… SUFFERED THE EXTREME PENALTY DURING THE REIGN OF TIBERIUS AT THE HANDS OF ONE OF OUR PROCURATORS, PONTIUS PILATUS"; the movement had reached Rome. Fire at 15.38–43. A NON-CHRISTIAN, HOSTILE witness — which strengthens rather than weakens his testimony that Christians existed and were punished.** **🛑 THREE CONSTRAINTS ON THE HYPOTHESIS, RECORDED AS PROMINENTLY AS ANY SUPPORT: (1) TACITUS GIVES NO DURATION AND NO END DATE — the familiar "64–68" is a CONSTRUCTED interval pairing an attested start with Nero's death, and using it to satisfy Revelation 13's 42 months would be exactly the rounding q170 forbids; (2) TACITUS DESCRIBES A PERSECUTION *AT ROME*, not an empire-wide administrative program, which sits awkwardly against Revelation 13:7's "every tribe and people and language and nation" and 13:16's "all, both small and great" — A REAL OPEN PROBLEM, and THE PRICE OF STAGE 40'S REFUSAL TO SHRINK THAT PHRASE, PAID RATHER THAN RENEGOTIATED; (3) TACITUS WROTE ROUGHLY HALF A CENTURY LATER — TIER C, NOT TIER A.** **STILL REQUIRED: the LATIN of 15.44 · Suetonius *Nero* 16, 38 · any source attesting the persecution CONTINUING to Nero's death · the precise date of the fire · any attestation of Neronian persecution OUTSIDE Rome · Pliny the Younger to Trajan on how imperial action against Christians actually worked.**

NEVER flatten "the apostles were martyred" into a uniform claim. AND DO NOT import q171's forbidden expectation into the reconstruction and then discover it there.**

That is a real narrowing of the search space, and it came from one adverb.**

214. **🆕 CONFINEMENT–FUNCTION DISTINCTION — supersedes the Binding-Scope Control alone. (raised 2026-08-21)** **CONDITION ✅: ἐκράτησεν SEIZED → ἔδησεν BOUND → ἔβαλεν εἰς τὴν ἄβυσσον THREW IN → ἔκλεισεν SHUT → ἐσφράγισεν ἐπάνω αὐτοῦ SEALED OVER HIM — and ✅ 20:7 calls it ἐκ τῆς φυλακῆς αὐτοῦ, HIS PRISON.** **FUNCTION ✅: ἵνα μὴ πλανήσῃ ἔτι τὰ ἔθνη — he may no longer deceive the nations during the thousand years.** **🛑 NEITHER MAY ERASE THE OTHER. BOTH EXTREMES FAIL: NOT "binding means absolutely no satanic influence whatsoever," and NOT "binding means practically nothing beyond a vague symbolic defeat." THE TEXT GIVES REAL CONFINEMENT WITH A SPECIFICALLY STATED FUNCTIONAL CONSEQUENCE.** **📌 The earlier Binding-Scope Control guarded only ONE side — it prevented over-expanding the restriction but NOT the opposite error of softening "bound" into mere inconvenience. THE GREEK MADE THE SECOND ERROR VISIBLE, SO THE RULE IS UPGRADED.** **⚠️ AND A RESTRAINT ON *dei*: the release is NECESSARY, but Revelation does NOT state Yah's MOTIVE — "Yah gives Satan another chance" is NOT available, because it supplies a reason the text withholds.**

215. **🛑🛑 THE FIRST-RESURRECTION ARGUMENT DEGRADES IN RVR1960 — the load-bearing finding is partially invisible in the most widely used Spanish Bible. (raised 2026-08-22, see [[02j-translation-architecture-the-spanish-edition-and-the-greek]])** **✅ SBLGNT: 20:4 καὶ ἔζησαν / 20:5 οὐκ ἔζησαν — THE SAME VERB. ✅ ESV preserves it. ✅ RVA-2015 PRESERVES it ("volvieron a vivir" / "no volvieron a vivir"). ❌ RVR1960 BREAKS it: 20:4 "y **vivieron**" but 20:5 "no **volvieron a vivir**."** **❗ A SPANISH READER USING RVR1960 MAY NEVER NOTICE THE PARALLEL THAT CARRIES THE ENTIRE ARGUMENT (q206).** **⚠️ NOT AN ERROR BY RVR1960 — "volver a vivir" is natural Spanish and varying it is a defensible translator's choice. THE PROBLEM IS THAT AN ARGUMENT BUILT ON *VERBAL IDENTITY* CANNOT SURVIVE A TRANSLATION THAT VARIES WORDING FOR STYLE. ANY ARGUMENT OF THAT CLASS IS TRANSLATION-FRAGILE AND MUST BE FLAGGED WHEREVER IT APPEARS.** **📌 AND THE ADVANTAGE SWITCHES INSIDE THE SAME CHAPTER: ✅ 20:6's ἐξουσία is **potestad** in RVR1960 (accurate — authority/jurisdiction) but "power" in ESV and "poder" in RVA-2015 (both lose it). ✅ Also RVR1960's 20:4 "recibieron **facultad** de juzgar" carries κρίμα ἐδόθη αὐτοῖς well. NO SINGLE TRANSLATION IN EITHER LANGUAGE IS RELIABLE ACROSS THE PASSAGE.** **⚠️ RETRIEVAL UNCERTAINTY, RECORDED NOT SMOOTHED: the RVA-2015 fetch of 20:3 ended without the "debe ser desatado" clause. ALMOST CERTAINLY TRUNCATION, NOT OMISSION — RE-RETRIEVE, and do NOT claim RVA-2015 omits it (S16).**

216. **🌐 TRANSLATION ARCHITECTURE — new rules, a glossary term, and an ADR-0008 AMENDMENT pending approval. (raised 2026-08-22, from the author's own question)** **✅ THE "MIGHT" PROBLEM DOES NOT EXIST IN SPANISH: RVR1960's "para que no **engañase** más a las naciones" is *para que* + IMPERFECT SUBJUNCTIVE, doing NATIVELY what ἵνα μὴ + aorist subjunctive does, because Spanish retains the productive subjunctive English largely lost. AT THAT VERSE SPANISH IS STRUCTURALLY CLOSER TO THE GREEK THAN ENGLISH IS. ("hasta que **fuesen** cumplidos" likewise matches ἄχρι **τελεσθῇ**; "más" renders **ἔτι**; "**debe** ser desatado" renders **δεῖ**.)** **📖 NEW GLOSSARY TERM — TRANSLATION ARTIFACT: a wording, ambiguity, emphasis or apparent theological problem CREATED OR AMPLIFIED BY A TRANSLATION CHOICE and not present in the same form in the source text. 🛑 MUST NOT BE TREATED AS SOURCE-TEXT EVIDENCE. Standing example: Revelation 20:3's English "might." 📌 Vocabulary correction the author requested: he reached for "terse," which means concise — the term he wanted is TRANSLATION ARTIFACT.** **🆕 RULES: SOURCE-LANGUAGE-FIRST TRANSLATION (re-check source → English witnesses → Spanish witnesses → WHETHER THE NOTE IS NEEDED IN SPANISH AT ALL) · TRANSLATION-DIVERGENCE NOTES ARE WRITTEN, NOT TRANSLATED · TRANSLATION-INDEPENDENT SOURCE ANCHORING (the Greek appears in EVERY edition — THE ONLY ELEMENT COMMON TO ALL EDITIONS) · NAME THE BASE TRANSLATION · 🏷️ SCOPE TAGS `SOURCE-LANGUAGE` / `ENGLISH-SPECIFIC` / `SPANISH-SPECIFIC`.** **🧭 ARCHITECTURE: ❌ NOT Greek → English book → translated Spanish book; ✅ BUT source spine → ENGLISH BRANCH *and* source spine → SPANISH BRANCH. The editions share FINDINGS and SOURCE TEXT, NOT translation commentary — so they will carry DIFFERENT NOTES AT DIFFERENT VERSES, which is CORRECT, NOT DEFECTIVE.** **⚠️ CORRECTION AGAINST OURSELVES: a draft ADR-0011 was prepared and **WITHDRAWN** — [[ADR-0008-source-language-first-localization]], accepted 2026-08-12 at Stage 17, ALREADY DECIDES THIS; its §5 already requires original-language analysis preserved IDENTICALLY across editions with each edition explaining its own translation. THE AUTHOR RE-DERIVED HIS OWN RULE INDEPENDENTLY FROM A LIVE PROBLEM, WHICH IS EVIDENCE IT IS RIGHT.** **📄 PROPOSED INSTEAD, PENDING APPROVAL — an AMENDMENT to ADR-0008 covering three things that genuinely exceed it: (1) ⚠️ THE RVR1960 DESIGNATION IS NOW CHALLENGED BY EVIDENCE — not a reason to switch, but a reason to DECIDE DELIBERATELY rather than by inheritance; likely third option is RVR1960 as base PLUS RVA-2015 as a second Spanish witness at translation-sensitive verses, exactly as ADR-0003 already carries ESV *and* CJB; (2) 🆕 a NARROW TRANSLATION-ARTIFACT EXCEPTION to ADR-0008's allowance that "explanatory prose may be translated and adapted"; (3) 🆕 the SCOPE-TAGGING SYSTEM, which makes ADR-0008's rule ENFORCEABLE rather than merely stated.** **⚠️ SCOPE WARNING: ONE PASSAGE IS NOT A TRANSLATION EVALUATION. RVR1909 · RVR1995 · NVI · LBLA still owed before any base is confirmed (S17).**

225. **🛑 DO NOT REOPEN REVELATION 20's TWO-STAGE STRUCTURE — and a counter-correction on "morally higher." DO NOT INFER THAT EVERY PERSON AT THE LATER JUDGMENT IS MORALLY INFERIOR IN A SIMPLE RANKED SENSE UNLESS THE TEXT ESTABLISHES IT.**

226. **🔑 THE COMPRESSION QUESTION — how do the broad resurrection texts map onto Revelation's two stages? (raised 2026-08-23)** **✅ John 5:28–29 VERIFIED: "an hour is coming when ALL WHO ARE IN THE TOMBS will hear his voice and come out" — to the resurrection of LIFE or of JUDGMENT. That sounds like the great-white-throne horizon more than a limited martyr resurrection.** **COMPRESSION HYPOTHESIS: John 5 may compress the entire resurrection/judgment reality into one "hour" while Revelation supplies internal stages — apocalyptic discourse demonstrably compresses extended periods (Visionary Compression Control, Stage 39). ⚠️ BUT WE MAY NOT ASSERT THE OPPOSITE EITHER.** **❗ AND JOHN 6 CREATES REAL PRESSURE: "I will raise him up ON THE LAST DAY," repeatedly. If the first resurrection precedes a thousand-year interval, WHAT DOES "THE LAST DAY" MEAN? Candidates: the final day of an age · the final resurrection horizon considered corporately · the actual final consummative day · language Revelation expands internally. 🛑 A GENUINE OBSTACLE TO CARELESS STAGING — needs source-language and Johannine-context work rather than treating "last day" as self-defining. JOHN 6 NOT RETRIEVED.** **📌 AND ✅ 1 Cor 15:23's "THOSE WHO BELONG TO CHRIST" sounds BROAD, not "some martyrs now, others a thousand years later." Options: the martyrs REPRESENT the faithful corporately · Paul COMPRESSES stages · or Revelation 20's population is BROADER than we read it — the disputed-syntax question already at q212.** **⚠️ AND ✅ Daniel 12:2 says "MANY of those who sleep in the DUST shall AWAKE," NOT explicitly "all" — preserve whether רַבִּים *rabbim* means many-but-not-everyone or functions corporately for the multitude. DO NOT IMPORT ENGLISH ARITHMETIC INTO HEBREW.**

227. **🛑 THE LIVING-TRANSFORMATION BURDEN AND THE SUPERNATURAL-POSSIBILITY BRAKE. (raised 2026-08-23)** **✅ 1 Corinthians 15:51–52 VERIFIED: "WE SHALL NOT ALL SLEEP, BUT WE SHALL ALL BE CHANGED, IN A MOMENT, IN THE TWINKLING OF AN EYE, at the last trumpet… the dead will be raised imperishable, AND WE SHALL BE CHANGED." *(The author's long-held "twinkling of an eye" memory CONFIRMED VERBATIM.)* ✅ 1 Thessalonians 4:16–17 VERIFIED: "THE DEAD IN CHRIST WILL RISE FIRST. Then we who are alive… will be CAUGHT UP together with them IN THE CLOUDS."** **❗ IF REVELATION 20's FIRST RESURRECTION IS IDENTIFIED WITH THESE, A HISTORICAL MODEL MAY REQUIRE *DEAD SAINTS RAISED* **AND** *LIVING SAINTS INSTANTANEOUSLY TRANSFORMED* — FAR HARDER TO HIDE HISTORICALLY THAN A PURELY HEAVENLY VINDICATION VISION. Unless: Paul and Revelation describe RELATED BUT NOT IDENTICAL events · the transformation removes participants from ordinary visibility · the event is geographically/covenantally localized · or the temporal relationship is misunderstood. THESE CANNOT BE SOLVED BY SPECULATION — THIS IS A FALSIFICATION CRITERION.** **⚠️ AND 1 THESSALONIANS' *πρῶτον* IS RELATIVE TO THE LIVING — the dead rise BEFORE the living are caught up WITHIN that sequence. DO NOT TREAT IT AS LEXICAL IDENTITY WITH REVELATION'S *ἀνάστασις ἡ πρώτη*. CORRESPONDENCE, NOT IDENTITY.** **🆕 SUPERNATURAL-POSSIBILITY BRAKE: a biblical precedent showing an event COULD occur supernaturally may answer an objection of IMPOSSIBILITY — but it DOES NOT CONSTITUTE EVIDENCE that the proposed historical event ACTUALLY OCCURRED. Transformed embodiment genuinely answers "raised saints could not have escaped notice"; IT WOULD BE VERY EASY TO LET THAT ANSWER DO DOUBLE DUTY AS EVIDENCE. IT MAY NOT.** **COROLLARY: ABSENCE OF EVIDENCE HAS WEIGHT ONLY RELATIVE TO THE AMOUNT AND TYPE OF EVIDENCE WE SHOULD REASONABLY EXPECT TO SURVIVE — ARGUED, NOT ASSERTED IN EITHER DIRECTION.**

228. **🔑🔑 REV 6 INTERMEDIATE-STATE CONTROL — the strongest argument in the stage, Revelation-internal, and it cuts AGAINST this project's convenience. (raised 2026-08-23)** **✅ Revelation 6:9 VERIFIED: "I saw under the altar THE SOULS OF THOSE WHO HAD BEEN SLAIN for the word of God and for the witness they had borne." ⚠️ vv.10–11 TRUNCATED (q224) — the "how long" cry, the white robes and "rest a little longer" are REPORTED ONLY, and they matter. Revelation 7 likewise places the faithful before the throne.** **❗❗ THE MARTYRS DID NOT NEED CHAPTER 20 IN ORDER TO EXIST CONSCIOUSLY AFTER DEATH. THEREFORE REVELATION 20's "THEY LIVED / FIRST RESURRECTION" MAY NOT BE REDUCED MERELY TO CONSCIOUS POSTMORTEM SURVIVAL WITHOUT EXPLAINING THE CHANGE OF STATE.** **THIS SUBSTANTIALLY WEAKENS THE COMMON SPIRITUAL-RESURRECTION SOLUTION — that "first resurrection" simply means martyrs went to heaven when they died. THEY ALREADY HAVE THAT IN CHAPTER 6.** **📌 AND IT CUTS AGAINST OUR OWN CONVENIENCE: A PAST MILLENNIUM WOULD BE ENORMOUSLY SIMPLER UNDER THE SPIRITUAL READING. THE PROJECT'S OWN TEXT REFUSES IT.** **⚠️ SECOND, INDEPENDENT ARGUMENT IN THE SAME DIRECTION IF IT HOLDS ON RE-RETRIEVAL: Hebrews 12:23's "THE SPIRITS OF THE RIGHTEOUS MADE PERFECT" describes righteous dead in conscious heavenly terms WITHOUT calling it resurrection — so CONSCIOUS HEAVENLY EXISTENCE ≠ AUTOMATICALLY RESURRECTION.** **STATUS: FIRST RESURRECTION ACTUAL-CHANGE PRESSURE = VERY HIGH.**

(raised 2026-08-23, see §3)** **✅ LXX: **ἐπιβλέψονται πρὸς μὲ ἀνθ' ὧν ΚΑΤΩΡΧΗΣΑΝΤΟ** ("because they MOCKED / danced in triumph over"). ✅ MT: **אֵת אֲשֶׁר־דָּקָרוּ** ("whom they PIERCED").** **❗ "MOCKED" AND "PIERCED" ARE MATERIALLY DIFFERENT READINGS OF THE SAME CLAUSE.** **❌ A RETRIEVAL AGENT SMOOTHED THIS INTO *"both point to the same theological event; verbal form differs."* **REJECTED — THAT IS PRECISELY THE EVIDENCE-TYPE COLLAPSE ADR-0010 FORBIDS.**** **📌 KEPT IN THE **SOURCE-TEXT** TALLY, NOT THE TRANSLATION TALLY — a translator choosing between senses and a source text reading differently are NOT the same phenomenon (methodology v2.3).** **STILL OWED: what John 19:37 and Revelation 1:7 do with this clause, since both cite it and either could follow MT, LXX, or neither.**

246. **❌❌ THEODOTION DANIEL 7:13–14 NOT RETRIEVED — LOAD-BEARING, AND IT DESTABILIZES A CLAIM WE ALREADY HOLD. (raised 2026-08-23, see §6)** **✅ RETRIEVED GREEK: **ἰδοὺ ΕΠΙ ΤΩΝ ΝΕΦΕΛΩΝ ΤΟΥ ΟΥΡΑΝΟΥ ὡς υἱὸς ἀνθρώπου ἤρχετο, καὶ ΩΣ ΠΑΛΑΙΟΣ ΗΜΕΡΩΝ ΠΑΡΗΝ.** ✅ ARAMAIC: **עִם־עֲנָנֵי שְׁמַיָּא** ("WITH the clouds"). ✅ MATTHEW 24:30: **ἐπὶ τῶν νεφελῶν** — **MATTHEW TRACKS THE GREEK PREPOSITION, NOT THE ARAMAIC.**** **❗❗ BUT THE SECOND CLAUSE IS THE PROBLEM: Aramaic and ESV give *"he came TO the Ancient of Days and was PRESENTED before him."* **THIS GREEK GIVES "AND AS AN ANCIENT OF DAYS WAS PRESENT."** MATERIALLY DIFFERENT — and the **ὡς** is the classic tell identifying the text as **OLD GREEK rather than Theodotion**, which is how the version was identified, since the source's own label was not independently confirmed.** **🛑 **q180 — that Daniel 7:13's son of man is PRESENTED BEFORE the Ancient of Days rather than descending to earth — RESTS ON THE ARAMAIC. THE OLD GREEK DOES NOT CLEARLY SAY IT.** A retrieval agent asserted "he approaches the throne" for the Old Greek; **THE TEXT IT QUOTED DOES NOT ESTABLISH THAT. ASSERTION REJECTED; DIVERGENCE RECORDED UNRESOLVED.**** **❗ THEODOTION IS NOW LOAD-BEARING — *WHICH GREEK DANIEL MATTHEW TRACKS* IS EXACTLY THE QUESTION AND WE HAVE ONLY ONE OF THE TWO. **DISCHARGE AT STAGE 49**, where Daniel 7 is already on the comparison list.**

247. **❌❌ EVAL CASE — A FABRICATED GREEK PARALLEL, CAUGHT BEFORE ENTRY. Caught only by reading the table against the verse rather than against itself.** **TWO FURTHER MISLABELS FROM OTHER LANES, SAME CLASS: ❌ Deuteronomy's **συνάξει** graded "VERBAL IDENTICAL" against **ἐπισυνάξουσιν** — they are not identical, and that difference IS q243 · ❌ Matthew's **ὄψονται** graded "same root ὁράω-family" as **ἐπιβλέψονται** — **ἐπιβλέπω is from βλέπω. DIFFERENT ROOTS.**** **📌 STANDING EVAL: any future retrieval table must be checked ROW BY ROW AGAINST THE ACTUAL VERSE. **THE *DELEGATED RETRIEVAL IS RETRIEVAL, NOT JUDGEMENT* RULE WAS WRITTEN ONE STAGE EARLIER AND PAID FOR ITSELF IMMEDIATELY.**

248. **⚠️ συντέλεια τοῦ αἰῶνος — five occurrences, and the Matthew 13 ⟷ Matthew 24 hinge is thinner than the tranche assumed. 249. **🛑🛑 MATTHEW 24:36's περὶ δέ — THE STRONGEST OUTSTANDING COUNTER-ARGUMENT TO THIS PROJECT'S OWN PROMOTION, AND THE TRANCHE DID NOT ENGAGE IT AT ALL.
