251. **🔑🔑🔑 γενεά IN MATTHEW — THE DEMONSTRATIVE PATTERN, AND MATTHEW 23:36 IS THE STRONGEST EVIDENCE IN THE STAGE. (raised 2026-08-23, see §8)** **✅ ALL THIRTEEN OCCURRENCES RETRIEVED. **CONTEMPORARY BODY (8):** 11:16 · 12:39 · 12:41 · 12:42 · 12:45 · 16:4 · 17:17 · 23:36. **GENEALOGICAL COUNTING-UNIT (4):** 1:17 ×4. **DISPUTED (1):** 24:34.** **❗❗ AND 23:36 CARRIES THE SAME PHRASE-PAIR AS 24:34, ONE CHAPTER EARLIER: ✅ 23:36 **ἈΜΗΝ ΛΕΓΩ ΥΜΙΝ, ΠΑΝΤΑ ΤΑΥΤΑ ἥξει ἐπὶ ΤΗΝ ΓΕΝΕΑΝ ΤΑΥΤΗΝ** ⟷ ✅ 24:34 **ἈΜΗΝ ΛΕΓΩ ΥΜΙΝ ὅτι οὐ μὴ παρέλθῃ Η ΓΕΝΕΑ ΑΥΤΗ ἕως ἂν ΠΑΝΤΑ ΤΑΥΤΑ γένηται.** **THREE SHARED ELEMENTS — AND AT 23:36 THE REFERENT IS BEYOND DISPUTE: the scribes and Pharisees on whom comes all the righteous blood from Abel to Zechariah.**** **★ THE PRECISE FINDING, SHARPER THAN EITHER THE TRANCHE OR THE RETRIEVAL STATED: **IN MATTHEW, γενεά + A DEMONSTRATIVE DENOTES A CONTEMPORARY BODY IN *EVERY* INSTANCE WHERE THE REFERENT IS DETERMINABLE. ✅ MATTHEW 1:17's GENEALOGICAL USE CARRIES *NO DEMONSTRATIVE* (αἱ γενεαί, plain).**** **⚠️ HONEST COUNTERWEIGHT, SAME TYPEFACE: 1:17's four uses mean the word's range in Matthew GENUINELY EXCEEDS "contemporaries."** **❌ BUT A RETRIEVAL AGENT INFERRED FROM THIS THAT THE "RACE/PEOPLE" READING OF 24:34 IS LESS BURDENSOME — **NON SEQUITUR. GENEALOGICAL-COUNTING-UNIT IS NOT ETHNIC-RACE.** 1:17 BROADENS THE RANGE; IT DOES NOT SUPPLY *THAT* ESCAPE ROUTE. **THE "RACE/ETHNIC PEOPLE" SENSE IS ATTESTED IN BROADER GREEK AND IS *NOT DEMONSTRATED ANYWHERE IN MATTHEW*.**** **⚠️ AND THE TRANSLATION DIVERGENCE STANDS: ✅ CJB renders 24:34 "this **PEOPLE**" (ADR-0003) — which is why the finding is stated as *undemonstrated in Matthew* rather than *impossible*.** **OWED: a lexical authority for ἡ γενεὰ αὕτη specifically (S21-class); independent re-verification that the 13-occurrence inventory is exhaustive.**

252. **🆕 THE INTERTEXTUAL EXPANSION RULE — adopted WITH TWO LEASHES, and the leashes are part of the rule. (raised 2026-08-23, see §11)** **THE RULE, AS PROPOSED BY THE TRANCHE: *a later inspired text may elaborate the mechanics of an earlier prophetic image without invalidating that image's original Tanakh background.*** **ADOPTED — and this stage's own evidence supports it: ✅ Isaiah 27 supplies Matthew's TRUMPET without supplying his GATHERING VERB, which is exactly what "same image, developed vocabulary" looks like (q243).** **🛑 **LEASH 1 — ANTI-RESCUE: IF A READING NEEDS "IT IS BOTH" TO SURVIVE ITS WORST DATUM, THAT IS A FAILING CANDIDATE, NOT A LAYERED ONE.** Same shape as the Composite-Symbol leash (Stage 43) and Spiral Progression's (Stage 44). A rule permitting both readings simultaneously can absorb ANY counterevidence, and a rule explaining every arrangement explains none.** **🛑 **LEASH 2 — IT MAY NOT RUN INTERTEXTUAL PRIORITY BACKWARDS.** This project's Rule 2 already states: *never run it backwards — a LATER text developing an earlier one is not evidence about what the EARLIER one meant.* **THEREFORE PAUL MAY *ELABORATE* MATTHEW; PAUL IS NEVER *EVIDENCE FOR WHAT MATTHEW MEANT*. THE EXPANSION RUNS FORWARD ONLY.**** **📌 THE JOINING MUST BE DEMONSTRATED TEXT BY TEXT. **THE RULE LICENSES THE QUESTION, NEVER THE ANSWER.**

253. **🔑🔑 REVELATION 1:7 CLOSES q245 — NEITHER MATTHEW NOR JOHN FOLLOWS THE LXX AT ZECHARIAH 12:10. (raised 2026-08-24, see [[02p-caught-up-to-meet-the-lord]] §6)** **✅ SBLGNT VERIFIED: Ἰδοὺ ἔρχεται μετὰ τῶν νεφελῶν, καὶ ΟΨΕΤΑΙ αὐτὸν πᾶς ὀφθαλμὸς καὶ οἵτινες αὐτὸν ΕΞΕΚΕΝΤΗΣΑΝ, καὶ ΚΟΨΟΝΤΑΙ ἐπ' αὐτὸν ΠΑΣΑΙ ΑΙ ΦΥΛΑΙ ΤΗΣ ΓΗΣ.** **THREE FINDINGS: (1) **ἐξεκέντησαν IS THE HEBREW READING** (דָּקָרוּ, "pierced"), **NOT the LXX's κατωρχήσαντο** ("mocked") — Revelation does not follow the LXX here; (2) **BOTH Matthew 24:30 and Revelation 1:7 use ὁράω where LXX Zechariah uses ἐπιβλέπω** — which EXPLAINS the mismatch flagged at q242: Matthew's seeing-verb never matched because **the NT tradition here isn't following the LXX at all**; (3) **Rev 1:7 and Matt 24:30 share κόψονται πᾶσαι αἱ φυλαὶ τῆς γῆς NEAR-VERBATIM**.** **📌 AND Rev 1:7 COMBINES DANIEL 7 (coming with clouds) AND ZECHARIAH 12 (the pierced one mourned) IN ONE SENTENCE — the same two intertexts Matthew 24:30 combines.** **⚠️ WHAT IT DOES NOT ESTABLISH: that Matthew and John describe the same EVENT. It establishes a shared NON-LXX TEXTUAL TRADITION and a shared intertextual pairing. Correspondence–Identity still applies.** **OWED: John 19:37, which also cites Zechariah 12:10 — does it read ἐξεκέντησαν too?**

254. **🔑🔑 ἁρπάζω IS DESTINATION-NEUTRAL — Acts 8:39 is decisive. (raised 2026-08-24, see §2)** **✅ TWELVE simplex occurrences verified. FIVE state a destination; SEVEN do not.** **★ THE COUNTEREXAMPLE: ✅ Acts 8:39 πνεῦμα κυρίου ἥρπασεν τὸν Φίλιππον → ✅ 8:40 Φίλιππος δὲ εὑρέθη εἰς ΑΖΩΤΟΝ — **an ordinary coastal town of Judea, from which he walks on to Caesarea.** Against ✅ Rev 12:5's πρὸς τὸν θεὸν καὶ πρὸς τὸν θρόνον αὐτοῦ and ✅ 2 Cor 12:2's ἕως τρίτου οὐρανοῦ.** **❗ THE DESTINATION COMES FROM CONTEXT — A PREPOSITIONAL PHRASE OR A FOLLOWING NARRATIVE — IN EVERY CASE. THE VERB SUPPLIES NONE.** **🆕 RAPTURE-SYSTEM BRAKE: the term is legitimate ("rapture" derives from the Latin rendering of ἁρπάζω), but SECRECY · INVISIBILITY · A FIXED HEAVENLY STAY · A SEVEN-YEAR CHRONOLOGY are propositions LATER SYSTEMS ADD. Each was tested against the retrieved occurrences and EACH ANSWERED NO — secrecy fails at Acts 8:40, invisibility fails there too, heavenly destination fails at Acts 8:39, and permanence at 1 Thess 4:17 is carried by πάντοτε σὺν κυρίῳ, a SEPARATE CLAUSE.** **📌 ἁρπαγμός (Phil 2:6) is a DIFFERENT WORD — a noun — and is NOT merged into the count.**

255. **🔑 ἀπάντησις CARRIES NO ESCORT SENSE — the escort is in the narrative, never the noun. (raised 2026-08-24, see §3)** **✅ THREE occurrences: Matt 25:6 · Acts 28:15 · 1 Thess 4:17.** **✅ MATTHEW 25:6's return IS real — but it is at ✅ 25:10, ΕΙΣΗΛΘΟΝ ΜΕΤ' ΑΥΤΟΥ ΕΙΣ ΤΟΥΣ ΓΑΜΟΥΣ: **A DIFFERENT VERSE WITH A DIFFERENT VERB (εἰσέρχομαι). The noun did not carry it.** ✅ ACTS 28:15's greeters come out ἄχρι Ἀππίου Φόρου καὶ Τριῶν Ταβερνῶν and **THE TEXT NEVER SAYS THEY WENT BACK IN WITH PAUL** — that is inference, and a reasonable one.** **❗❗ ✅ AND 1 THESSALONIANS 4:17 STATES NO DIRECTION OF MOVEMENT AFTER THE MEETING AT ALL — no καταβησόμεθα, no ἀναβησόμεθα, no verb of travel. The next clause is RELATIONAL: καὶ οὕτως πάντοτε σὺν κυρίῳ ἐσόμεθα. **BOTH POPULAR MODELS ADD A MOVEMENT PAUL DID NOT WRITE.**** **★ AND THE COUNTEREXAMPLE FROM THE WORD-GROUP'S OWN USAGE: ✅ Matthew 8:34 — πᾶσα ἡ πόλις ἐξῆλθεν εἰς ὑπάντησιν τῷ Ἰησοῦ — **AND THE CITY THEN ASKS HIM TO LEAVE THEIR REGION. GOING OUT TO MEET SOMEONE DOES NOT EVEN ENTAIL WELCOME, LET ALONE ESCORT.**

A conclusion built on a non-exhaustive word list is not available.**

The claim is attested IN SCHOLARSHIP and unverified AT SOURCE.** **🛑 IT MAY NOT BE CITED AS ESTABLISHED IN EITHER DIRECTION — neither to support an escort-return model nor to dismiss one.** **OWED: the actual papyri and dated inscriptions; and the LXX's use of ἀπάντησις/ὑπάντησις for Hebrew קִרְאָה, which would show Greek-speaking Jewish usage rather than civic-ceremonial usage.** **📌 NOTE: the NT data ALONE already answers the question for this project's purposes (q255) — the noun does not carry the escort. 260. **🛑 ACTS 1:11 SPECIFIES MANNER AND NOTHING ELSE. (raised 2026-08-24, see §5)** **✅ VERIFIED: Acts 1:9 — ταῦτα εἰπὼν βλεπόντων αὐτῶν **ΕΠΗΡΘΗ**, καὶ **ΝΕΦΕΛΗ ΥΠΕΛΑΒΕΝ** αὐτὸν ἀπὸ τῶν ὀφθαλμῶν αὐτῶν *(ἐπήρθη aorist passive, agent unnamed; ὑπέλαβεν aorist active with **THE CLOUD as subject**)*. ✅ 1:11 — **ὃν τρόπον** ἐθεάσασθε αὐτὸν πορευόμενον εἰς τὸν οὐρανόν.** **❗ ὃν τρόπον SPECIFIES *MANNER*. IT DOES NOT SPECIFY LOCATION, VISIBILITY, AUDIENCE, OR TIMING.** **🛑 THEREFORE ACTS 1:11 MAY NOT BE MERGED WITH REVELATION 1:7's "EVERY EYE WILL SEE HIM." The universal-sight claim is Revelation's; the manner-claim is Acts'. DIFFERENT CLAIMS, NOT COMBINED WITHOUT AN ARGUMENT.** **✅ AND THE CLOUD PASSAGES DO NOT FLATTEN — FOUR PREPOSITIONS, THREE SUBJECTS: Acts 1:9 **THE CLOUD acts on Him** · Matt 24:30 Messiah **ΕΠΙ** them · 1 Thess 4:17 **BELIEVERS ΕΝ** them · Rev 1:7 Messiah **ΜΕΤΑ** them · Daniel 7:13 Aramaic **עִם**, Old Greek **ΕΠΙ**.**

261. **✅ THE LEXICAL PAIR EXISTS AT 2 THESSALONIANS 2:1 — and the evidence-categories are preserved verbatim so they cannot drift again. (raised 2026-08-24, see [[02q-2-thessalonians-2-parousia-gathering-lawless-one]] §2)** **✅ SBLGNT VERIFIED: Ἐρωτῶμεν δὲ ὑμᾶς, ἀδελφοί, ὑπὲρ τῆς **ΠΑΡΟΥΣΙΑΣ** τοῦ κυρίου ἡμῶν Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ **καὶ** ἡμῶν **ΕΠΙΣΥΝΑΓΩΓΗΣ** ἐπ' αὐτόν — two genitives joined by καί under a single ὑπέρ.** **❗ BOTH FACTS STAND TOGETHER AND NEITHER MAY BE DROPPED: **THE PAIR IS GENUINELY HERE** ✅ **AND IT IS GENUINELY NOT IN MATTHEW 24:31** ✅ *(παρουσία at Matt 24:3, 27, 37, 39 — nowhere inside 24:29–36)*.** **📌 CATEGORIES, LOCKED: **ἐπισυνάγω correspondence = LEXICAL · παρουσία correspondence = BROADER-DISCOURSE/CONTEXTUAL · the two COMBINED at Matthew 24:31 = NOT LEXICAL and must never be represented as such · 2 Thessalonians 2:1 = where the pair ACTUALLY exists.**** **⚠️ AND THE VERB-FAMILY LINK TO MATTHEW IS HELD UNDER q233: the ἐπισυναγ- family is **NOT inherently eschatological** (Hebrews 10:25 uses the noun for an ordinary assembly). Shared vocabulary establishes a shared WORD.**

262. **★ THERE IS NO VERB OF REMOVAL AT 2 THESSALONIANS 2:7. `ENGLISH-SPECIFIC` (raised 2026-08-24, see §4)** **✅ SBLGNT: μόνον ὁ κατέχων ἄρτι **ἕως ΕΚ ΜΕΣΟΥ ΓΕΝΗΤΑΙ**.** **❗ **γένηται IS FROM γίνομαι — "COME TO BE," "BECOME." IT IS NOT A PASSIVE OF αἴρω, λαμβάνω, OR ANY VERB OF TAKING.** Literally: *"until he comes to be out of the midst."*** **❗❗ THE SYSTEMS THAT SPEAK OF THE RESTRAINER BEING *TAKEN AWAY* OR *REMOVED* ARE IMPORTING A VERB PAUL DID NOT WRITE.** ESV's *"until he is out of the way"* is a defensible rendering of the sense; **"removed" is not in the Greek.** **⚠️ AND THE BRAKE RUNS BOTH WAYS: this does NOT establish that nothing is removed. It establishes that the text states a CHANGE OF POSITION, not an AGENT REMOVING ANYTHING. BOTH OVER-READINGS ARE BLOCKED.** **OWED: RVR1960/RVA-2015 at 2:7; a lexical authority for κατέχω; and every NT use of ἐκ μέσου.**

263. **⚠️ καταργέω SHARED BETWEEN 1 CORINTHIANS 15:26 AND 2 THESSALONIANS 2:8 — and the tenses differ. (raised 2026-08-24, see §4)** **✅ 1 Cor 15:26 — ἔσχατος ἐχθρὸς **ΚΑΤΑΡΓΕΙΤΑΙ** ὁ θάνατος — **PRESENT**, of **DEATH**. ✅ 2 Thess 2:8 — **ΚΑΤΑΡΓΗΣΕΙ** τῇ ἐπιφανείᾳ τῆς παρουσίας αὐτοῦ — **FUTURE**, of **THE LAWLESS ONE**.** **❗ SAME VERB, TWO ESCHATOLOGICAL OBJECTS, TWO TENSES. This bears directly on the Stage 48 observation about 15:26's present: **PAUL DEMONSTRABLY HAD THE FUTURE FORM AVAILABLE AND USED IT ELSEWHERE FOR AN ESCHATOLOGICAL DESTRUCTION.**** **🛑 STILL AN OBSERVATION, NOT AN ARGUMENT. What a Greek present implies against a Greek future in eschatological discourse **REQUIRES A GRAMMAR AUTHORITY THIS PROJECT DOES NOT POSSESS (S22).** Recorded as a LEXICAL FACT ONLY.** **📌 ALSO RECORDED: the **ἐνεργ- root runs three times** through 2 Thess 2 — ✅ 2:7 ἐνεργεῖται (the mystery) · 2:9 κατ' ἐνέργειαν τοῦ Σατανᾶ · 2:11 πέμπει… ἐνέργειαν πλάνης (**and GOD is the sender**). Intratextual observation; NO SYNTHESIS DRAWN.**

264. **⚠️ MATTHEW 24:12's ἀνομία — REPORTED, NOT VERIFIED. (raised 2026-08-24)** **The tranche asserts that Matthew 24:12 uses **ἀνομία**, the same lemma as 2 Thessalonians 2:3, 2:7 and 2:8's ἄνομος — and treats it as "genuinely lexical."** **❌ THIS STAGE'S RETRIEVAL OF MATTHEW 24:11–12 FAILED. THE LINK IS THEREFORE **REPORTED, NOT VERIFIED**, and may not be used as a lexical bridge until retrieved.** **📌 RECORDED BECAUSE IT WOULD BE VERY EASY TO CARRY FORWARD AS VERIFIED — it is adjacent to material that WAS verified, in a stage full of confirmed lemmas. **THAT ADJACENCY IS EXACTLY HOW AN UNVERIFIED CLAIM ACQUIRES BORROWED CREDIBILITY.**

265. **❌❌ THE ναός / ἱερόν / LXX / DANIEL LANE — UNRESOLVED RESEARCH DEBT, *NOT* A CONCLUSION. (raised 2026-08-24, see §1, §7)** **A retrieval lane assigned this audit returned **NOTHING** after 12+ dead sources (BibleHub, Blue Letter Bible, Katabiblon, Ellopos, Perseus, Archive.org rate-limited, Scripture4All PDF, Logos paywalled, two dead domains), writing: *"I will not invent Greek text or claim to have retrieved what I could not access."*** **✅ RECORDED AS THIS PROJECT'S FIRST EVAL-*POSITIVE* after three consecutive stages of eval-negatives — the rules produced the right failure.** **🛑 BUT THE FAILURE WAS *METHOD*, NOT SOURCE: the Bible Gateway SBLGNT pattern had already worked FIVE times (S11, S18, S28); **NEITHER FAILING LANE USED IT**, and one recorded the source as dry after getting English back. **2 Thessalonians 2:1–12 WAS THEN RETRIEVED FIRST TRY WITH THE KNOWN-GOOD PATTERN.**** **❗ THEREFORE **NO CONCLUSION ABOUT PAUL'S ναός IS AVAILABLE IN EITHER DIRECTION.** ❌ NOT AVAILABLE: *"Paul says ναὸς θεοῦ, therefore the Jerusalem sanctuary."* ❌ NOT AVAILABLE: *"Paul calls believers ναός elsewhere, therefore the community here."* **BOTH ARE THE SAME LEXICAL FALLACY REVERSED — a shape this project has now blocked four times (Anti-Replacement Principle · Anti-Inversion Brake · Removal ≠ Replacement · Lexical-Family Scope Test).**** **✅ ALL THAT IS VERIFIED: 2:4 reads **εἰς ΤΟΝ ΝΑΟΝ ΤΟΥ ΘΕΟΥ** — ναός, **WITH the definite article**, NOT ἱερόν — with **καθίσαι** (aorist infinitive) and **ἀποδεικνύντα** (present participle).** **THE EXACT WORK OWED: every ναός in the Pauline corpus · **whether Paul ever uses ἱερόν at all** · ναός of the Jerusalem building vs of the community, with verses for each · **LXX ναός and which Hebrew terms it renders** (הֵיכָל · בַּיִת · מִקְדָּש) · whether LXX ναός denotes the inner sanctuary specifically · Daniel's sanctuary and desecration vocabulary in Hebrew/Aramaic **AND BOTH GREEK VERSIONS** · and whether **Matthew 24:15's τὸ βδέλυγμα τῆς ἐρημώσεως is a VERBAL or merely THEMATIC match with Greek Daniel.**

266. **✅✅ παρουσία IS USED OF THE LAWLESS ONE — decisive for the project's lexical discipline. (raised 2026-08-24, see §5)** **✅ SBLGNT 2 Thess 2:9 — **οὗ ἐστιν Η ΠΑΡΟΥΣΙΑ κατ' ἐνέργειαν τοῦ Σατανᾶ** ἐν πάσῃ δυνάμει καὶ σημείοις καὶ τέρασιν ψεύδους.** **❗❗ **παρουσία IS NOT LEXICALLY RESERVED FOR MESSIAH.** It means presence / arrival / coming, and **CONTEXT SUPPLIES WHOSE AND WHAT KIND.**** **📌 THIS RETROACTIVELY VINDICATES THE **COMING-LANGUAGE CONTROL** (Stage 33), which was built on Revelation 2:5 (come and remove ITS lampstand), 2:16 (war against THOSE offenders) and **3:3 (IF YOU WILL NOT WAKE UP, I will come like a thief)** — repeated, conditional, non-final comings. **THE SAME DISCIPLINE NOW HOLDS IN PAUL, FROM AN INDEPENDENT AUTHOR AND A DIFFERENT GENRE.**** **📌 CONSEQUENCE: the question is never *"does the parousia occur here?"* but **"WHOSE parousia, and WHAT KIND?"** — and that applies to every παρουσία in the project, including Matthew 24:3, 27, 37, 39.**

267. **🆕 THE FUTURE-SANCTUARY BRAKE — written BEFORE the data arrives, so it cannot be tuned afterward. (raised 2026-08-24, see §7)** **✅ PAUL DOES NOT SAY "REBUILT." He does not say another sanctuary will be constructed. 2 Thessalonians 2:4 says only **εἰς τὸν ναὸν τοῦ θεοῦ καθίσαι**.** **🛑 **A FUTURE REBUILT SANCTUARY MAY NOT BE INSERTED INTO 2 THESSALONIANS 2 BECAUSE A LATER THEOLOGICAL CHRONOLOGY REQUIRES ONE. IT NEEDS INDEPENDENT TEXTUAL GROUNDS.**** **⚖️ AND SYMMETRICALLY, BECAUSE THE OPPOSITE ERROR IS EQUALLY AVAILABLE: **THAT A SANCTUARY STOOD WHEN PAUL WROTE DOES NOT ITSELF SETTLE HIS REFERENT.** His own Corinthian usage prevents that shortcut. **NEITHER THE HISTORICAL SETTING NOR THE METAPHORICAL PRECEDENT DECIDES THE VERSE; ONLY THE AUDIT (q265) CAN.**** **📌 WRITTEN NOW DELIBERATELY: the brake exists BEFORE the retrieval that will bear on it, on the same principle as the Universal-Language Scope Test's clause (c) and the Until-Reversal Caution — **a rule composed after the answer starts to matter cannot be trusted.**

268. **🆕 THE RESTRAINER RULE — a refusal, not an identification. (raised 2026-08-24, see §5)** **✅ VERIFIED: 2 Thess 2:6 — **ΤΟ ΚΑΤΕΧΟΝ** *(neuter)* · 2:7 — **Ο ΚΑΤΕΧΩΝ** *(masculine)*. **THE NEUTER-TO-MASCULINE SHIFT IS REAL, CONFIRMED IN GREEK.**** **🛑 **DO NOT IDENTIFY THE RESTRAINER BECAUSE A CANDIDATE FITS A THEOLOGICAL SYSTEM.** Any candidate must account for **ALL FOUR**: (1) the **NEUTER** form at 2:6 · (2) the **MASCULINE** form at 2:7 · (3) that ✅ **PAUL'S FIRST-CENTURY READERS ALREADY KNEW THE REFERENT** (2:5 — ἔτι ὢν πρὸς ὑμᾶς ταῦτα ἔλεγον ὑμῖν; 2:6 — τὸ κατέχον **ΟΙΔΑΤΕ**) · (4) the **ἐκ μέσου γένηται** condition, which is a **CHANGE OF POSITION, NOT A REMOVAL** (q262).** **❗❗ **THIS STAGE PROPOSES NO CANDIDATE. NONE.**** **📌 AND THE EPISTEMIC POINT THAT TRAVELS WITH IT: ✅ 2:5 STATES THAT PAUL HAD ALREADY TAUGHT THIS ORALLY. **WE POSSESS THE LETTER; THEY POSSESSED LETTER + PRIOR INSTRUCTION.** Some ambiguity here may be **IRRECOVERABLE**, and manufacturing certainty to compensate for missing information is exactly what the Revelatory-Limit Control (Stage 38) forbids in its own domain.**

269. **⚠️ 1 CORINTHIANS 6:19 — INDIVIDUAL OR CORPORATE? DISPUTED, and a retrieval conclusion was OVERRULED. (raised 2026-08-24, see [[02r-what-temple-did-paul-mean]] §1)** **✅ SBLGNT: ἢ οὐκ οἴδατε ὅτι **τὸ σῶμα ὑμῶν ναὸς** τοῦ ἐν ὑμῖν ἁγίου πνεύματός ἐστιν;** **A retrieval lane concluded the verse is *"NOT applying the temple metaphor to an individual body"* but to *"the community's collective body,"* reasoning from the plural pronouns.** **❌ NON SEQUITUR, REJECTED: **σῶμα is SINGULAR while ὑμῶν is PLURAL**, which reads naturally as **DISTRIBUTIVE** ("the body of each of you") — and **1 Corinthians 6:15–20 is about an individual joining himself to a prostitute.** PLURAL ADDRESS DOES NOT MAKE THE σῶμα COLLECTIVE.** **📌 AND IT MATTERS: **if 6:19 is individual, Paul has THREE ναός senses — community, individual body, and the disputed 2 Thess 2:4 — not two.** The lane's flattening would have hidden a third sense.** **RECORDED AS DISPUTED. OWED: a commentary or grammar authority; and 1 Cor 6:15–20 retrieved in full.**

270. **⚠️ THE PAULINE ναός INVENTORY IS NOT EXHAUSTIVE. (raised 2026-08-24)** **✅ SIX occurrences retrieved and verified: 1 Cor 3:16 · 3:17 · 6:19 · 2 Cor 6:16 · Eph 2:21 · 2 Thess 2:4.** **❌ NOT SEARCHED: Romans · Philippians · Galatians · Colossians · 1–2 Timothy · Titus · Philemon.** **❗ THE "FIVE-OF-SIX METAPHORICAL, NEVER THE JERUSALEM BUILDING" FIGURE — WHICH CARRIES THE STAGE'S VERDICT — IS REPORTED AT THAT LIMIT AND MUST BE LABELLED SO WHEREVER IT IS USED.** **⚠️ AND THE SAME GAP APPLIES TO ἱερόν: only 1 Cor 9:13 was confirmed in Paul; the remaining letters were not swept. **A COMPLETE PAULINE SWEEP OF BOTH WORDS IS OWED BEFORE THE VERDICT IS LEANED ON FURTHER.**

271. **⚠️ 2 CORINTHIANS 6:16's TANAKH QUOTATION — REPORTED, NOT LXX-VERIFIED. (raised 2026-08-24)** **✅ SBLGNT: ἡμεῖς γὰρ ναὸς θεοῦ ἐσμεν ζῶντος· καθὼς εἶπεν ὁ θεὸς ὅτι **Ἐνοικήσω ἐν αὐτοῖς καὶ ἐμπεριπατήσω, καὶ ἔσομαι αὐτῶν θεός, καὶ αὐτοὶ ἔσονταί μου λαός**.** **⚠️ SOURCE GIVEN AS **Leviticus 26:12 / Exodus 29:45** BY AN ESV CROSS-REFERENCE — **NOT VERIFIED AGAINST THE LXX**.** **❗ PER *QUOTATION BEHAVIOR IS PER-QUOTATION* (v2.3), no generalization from Paul's other quotations is permitted here: Romans 10:18 quotes the LXX verbatim while Romans 11:26 matches neither MT nor LXX. **THIS ONE MUST BE CHECKED ON ITS OWN.**** **OWED: LXX Leviticus 26:12 and Exodus 29:45, and whether Ezekiel 37:27 is also in play.**

272. **❌❌ DOES THE LXX EVER USE ναός METAPHORICALLY FOR A *PEOPLE*? — THE SINGLE MOST DECISIVE OUTSTANDING ITEM. (raised 2026-08-24, see §3)** **✅ LXX ναός IS VERIFIED AS AN ACTUAL SANCTUARY THROUGHOUT: 3 Kingdoms 6 · Ezekiel 41:1, 41:4 · **Psalm 5:7 προσκυνήσω πρὸς ναὸν ἅγιόν σου** · **Habakkuk 2:20 ὁ δὲ κύριος ἐν ναῷ ἁγίῳ αὐτοῦ** · Joel 4:5 *(plural, of pagan temples)*.** **★ AND 3 KINGDOMS 6 STRATIFIES THREE TERMS: **οἶκος** (the house overall) → **ναός** (the inner sanctuary, rendering **הֵיכָל** *heikhal*) → **δαβείρ** (the innermost chamber, **דְּבִיר** *devir*).** **❌ BUT WHETHER THE LXX EVER APPLIES ναός TO A PEOPLE WAS **NOT RETRIEVED** — the lane stated plainly it would require an exhaustive LXX search it did not perform.** **❗❗ **EITHER ANSWER MOVES THE VERDICT.** If the LXX NEVER does it, Paul's community-as-ναός is a **PAULINE INNOVATION** — which makes its density in his own letters MORE striking, not less. If the LXX DOES do it, the community reading gains a **Tanakh-side precedent**.** **📌 ALSO RECORDED: ✅ **Daniel 5:2–3 LXX uses οἶκος τοῦ θεοῦ, NOT ναός**, for the Temple-vessel episode — relevant to the Stage 51 Belshazzar material. ✅ **And ἱερόν DOES occur in the LXX: Daniel 9:27's ἐπὶ τὸ ἱερόν.** ⚠️ A full LXX ἱερόν survey was not completed.**

273. **🔑🔑 MATTHEW 24:15 QUOTES DANIEL 12:11 LXX *VERBATIM* — and Daniel's own Greek VARIES. (raised 2026-08-24, see §4)** **✅ VERIFIED, AND THE VARIATION IS THE FINDING: **Daniel 9:27 — βδέλυγμα ΤΩΝ ΕΡΗΜΩΣΕΩΝ** (PLURAL genitive, NO article) · **Daniel 11:31 — βδέλυγμα ἐρημώσεως** (singular genitive, NO article) · **Daniel 12:11 — ΤΟ βδέλυγμα ΤΗΣ ἐρημώσεως** (singular genitive, **WITH the article**).** **✅ Matthew 24:15 — Ὅταν οὖν ἴδητε **ΤΟ ΒΔΕΛΥΓΜΑ ΤΗΣ ΕΡΗΜΩΣΕΩΣ** τὸ ῥηθὲν διὰ Δανιὴλ τοῦ προφήτου ἑστὸς ἐν τόπῳ ἁγίῳ.** **❗❗ **MATCHES 12:11 WORD FOR WORD, ARTICLE INCLUDED. MATCHES NEITHER 9:27 NOR 11:31.** THIS IS A VERBAL QUOTATION, NOT A THEMATIC ALLUSION, AND IT IS *SPECIFIC*: when Yeshua says "spoken of through Daniel the prophet," the Greek points at ONE VERSE.** **📌 AND DANIEL 12:11 IS THE VERSE PAIRING THE ABOMINATION WITH THE REMOVAL OF THE **תָּמִיד** AND A DAY-COUNT (**1,290**). A SUBSTANTIAL LEAD INTO STAGE 51 — AND IT AROSE FROM RETRIEVAL, NOT FROM A THEORY.** **⚠️ BRAKE: this establishes WHICH Daniel text Matthew quotes. It establishes NOTHING about what Matthew takes it to REFER TO, and nothing about Antiochus. Those are Stage 51's questions.** **⚠️ AND THE VERSION CAVEAT: the Daniel Greek obtained is **OLD GREEK (Swete)**. ❌ **THEODOTION FAILED A SECOND TIME (q246).** Whether Theodotion's 12:11 also matches Matthew is **UNKNOWN AND MATTERS.**

274. **❌ MATTHEW 24:15 AND 2 THESSALONIANS 2:4 SHARE *NO* VOCABULARY — the convergence is thematic, not lexical. (raised 2026-08-24, see §5)** **CHECKED WORD BY WORD, BOTH DIRECTIONS: **βδέλυγμα** ✅Matt ❌Paul · **ἐρημώσεως** ✅Matt ❌Paul · **ἀντικείμενος** ❌Matt ✅Paul · **ὑπεραιρόμενος** ❌Matt ✅Paul · **ναός** ❌Matt ✅Paul · **θεός** ❌Matt ✅Paul.** **❗ **MATTHEW DOES NOT EVEN USE ναός — HE USES τόπος ἅγιος** (ἑστὸς ἐν τόπῳ ἁγίῳ).** **THEREFORE THE PROPOSED "INTERTEXTUAL CONVERGENCE" BETWEEN THE TWO PASSAGES IS **THEMATIC**, AND THE TRANCHE'S PHRASING IMPLIED A STRONGER CLASS THAN THE DATA SUPPORTS. **THE CONVERGENCE IS REAL; ITS EVIDENCE CLASS IS WEAKER.**

275. **❌ ὑψόω vs ὑπεραίρω — PAUL'S SELF-EXALTATION LANGUAGE IS NOT VERBALLY DANIELIC. (raised 2026-08-24, see §6)** **✅ LXX Daniel 11:36 uses **ΥΨΟΩ** (ὑψωθήσεται). ✅ 2 Thessalonians 2:4 uses **ΥΠΕΡΑΙΡΩ** (ὑπεραιρόμενος). **ὑπεραίρω DOES NOT APPEAR IN LXX DANIEL 11:36.**** **❗ SO *"PAUL SOUNDS DANIELIC"* IS **TRUE AT THE LEVEL OF THEME AND FALSE AT THE LEVEL OF WORDING.** The self-exaltation correspondence is genuine and it is NOT a quotation.** **⚠️ AND THE HEBREW IS UNUSABLE: the lane returned Daniel 11:36's Hebrew *"reconstructed from Strong's refs"* — **THAT IS NOT A RETRIEVAL, AND IT IS QUOTED NOWHERE IN THE STAGE.** *(Same discipline as the scrambled Isaiah Hebrew at 48½ and the RVA-2015 truncation at 45¾.)*** **📌 ALSO VERIFIED AND FEEDING STAGE 51: ✅ Daniel 8:11–13 and 11:31 use **θυσία** for the removed **תָּמִיד**; ✅ Daniel 9:27 adds **σπονδή** (drink offering).** **OWED: the Hebrew of Daniel 11:36 properly retrieved; and whether Theodotion 11:36 uses ὑπεραίρω where the Old Greek does not — **which would reverse this finding.**

276. **♻️⬇️ THE MODERATE-TO-STRONG VERDICT ON 2 THESS 2:4 IS *WITHDRAWN*. (raised 2026-08-24, see §0, §7)** **THE TRANCHE RATED THE PHYSICAL-JERUSALEM-SANCTUARY READING **MODERATE-TO-STRONG**. ❌ **NOT SUPPORTED BY THE RETRIEVAL.**** **❗❗ **THE STRONGEST SINGLE ARGUMENT RUNS THE OTHER WAY: IN PAUL'S LETTERS ναός IS FIVE-OF-SIX METAPHORICAL AND *NEVER ONCE* THE JERUSALEM BUILDING — AND WHERE HE MEANS ACTUAL TEMPLE SERVICE HE SWITCHES TO ἱερόν (✅ 1 Cor 9:13). AUTHOR'S-OWN-ATTESTED-USAGE IS THE STRONGEST EVIDENCE CLASS THIS PROJECT RECOGNIZES (point 4 of the 12-point structure).**** **AND TWO SUPPORTING LEGS COLLAPSED: the **ναός/ἱερόν distinction is NEUTRAL** (it separates sanctuary from precinct, not Jerusalem from community — **both candidates are sanctuaries**), and **Matthew↔Paul shares zero vocabulary** (q274).** **📐 **REVISED VERDICT: OPEN.** COMMUNITY reading favoured by **INTRATEXTUAL** evidence; PHYSICAL reading favoured by **CONTEXTUAL** evidence *(a sanctuary stood when Paul wrote · ✅ 2:5's audience-recognition — they were expected to RECOGNIZE the referent · καθίσαι's spatial reading · the Danielic theme)*. **THESE ARE DIFFERENT AND NON-COMMENSURABLE EVIDENCE CLASSES AND ARE NOT SETTLED BY TALLY. NEITHER READING IS CLOSED.**** **🛑 **AND THIS CUTS AGAINST THIS PROJECT'S PREFERENCE — a physical first-century sanctuary would have tightened the chain considerably. THE DOWNGRADE IS RECORDED AS PROMINENTLY AS A PROMOTION WOULD HAVE BEEN, per the Constraint-Not-Resemblance Standard.** **📌 THE ROUTE THAT WAS AVAILABLE AND IS DECLINED IN WRITING SO IT CANNOT BE TAKEN QUIETLY LATER: *lean on the historical setting, lean on καθίσαι, call the Danielic parallel verbal, and move on.* **THE DANIELIC PARALLEL IS ὑψόω AGAINST ὑπεραίρω, AND THE HISTORICAL SETTING IS EVIDENCE ABOUT PAUL'S WORLD, NOT ABOUT PAUL'S VOCABULARY.**

239. **🔑 THE TRUMPET COMPARISON — one event, or independent uses of shared imagery? *(flagged Stage 11, 2026-08-11; investigated Stage 48, 2026-08-23)*** **THE ORIGINAL CHARGE: compare Isaiah 27:13 · Matthew 24:31 · 1 Corinthians 15:52 · 1 Thessalonians 4:16 · Revelation's trumpets, refusing BOTH shortcuts — cannot align Matthew with Isaiah while ignoring Paul, and cannot assume shared imagery means shared event.** **STAGE 48 RESULT, PARTIAL: ✅ Matthew's **μετὰ σάλπιγγος μεγάλης** · ✅ 1 Thess 4:16's **ἐν σάλπιγγι θεοῦ** · ✅ 1 Cor 15:52's **ἐν τῇ ἐσχάτῃ σάλπιγγι** · ✅ Rev 11:15's **ὁ ἕβδομος ἄγγελος ἐσάλπισεν** — ALL RETRIEVED, ALL DIFFERENT FORMULATIONS. **NO TWO OF THEM USE THE SAME PHRASE.**** **🛑 LAST ≠ SEVENTH REFUSED (q232), AND THE SEVENTH TRUMPET'S OWN VOCABULARY POINTS AT THE GREAT WHITE THRONE INSTEAD.** **❌ STILL OPEN AND BLOCKING: **ISAIAH 27:13 IN THE LXX WAS NOT RETRIEVED** (q231). Until it is, whether Matthew's "great trumpet" verbally tracks Isaiah's is UNDETERMINED — and that is the hinge of the whole comparison.** **📌 STAGE 11'S FRAMING VINDICATED: "the elaborateness of any required exceptions is itself evidence." So far NO elaborate exception has been needed — but neither has an identity been established.**

240. **❓ WHO ARE "THE ELECT" IN MATTHEW 24:31? *(flagged Stage 11, 2026-08-11; re-opened Stage 48, 2026-08-23 — STILL UNRESOLVED)*** **Scattered Israel alone, or the renewed covenant people centred on Messiah including the nations? *ἐκλεκτός* has deep Israel roots (Isaiah 45:4, 65:9) and is applied to Messiah's people at Romans 8:33 and Colossians 3:12.** **❗ STAGE 48 ADDS ONE DATUM AND IT PULLS TOWARD THE FIRST OPTION: ✅ Matthew's ONLY other use of ἐπισυνάγω is 23:37's **τὰ τέκνα σου** — *JERUSALEM'S CHILDREN* (q236). If the gathering verb is covenantal and city-directed one chapter earlier, "the elect" at 24:31 is under pressure to be read covenantally too.** **🛑 NOT DECIDED — and it MUST NOT be decided from the verb alone (two occurrences are a datum, not a rule), nor from ✅ 24:31's own scope phrase "ἐκ τῶν τεσσάρων ἀνέμων ἀπ' ἄκρων οὐρανῶν ἕως τῶν ἄκρων αὐτῶν," which is DIASPORA-RESTORATION language *(Deut 30:4, Zech 2:6)* whose LXX remains unretrieved (q231).** **OWED: every Matthean use of ἐκλεκτός · the LXX of the Isaiah election texts · and the Universal-Language Scope Test run on the four-winds phrase.**

## Bookmarked — Flagged for Later, Not Yet Investigated

- **🕊️ THE FAITH BRANCH — Biblical Epistemology. (marked 2026-08-24, DELIBERATELY NOT OPENED.)** **THE QUESTION: *what does Scripture mean by faith, and what relationship does biblical faith have to testimony, evidence, knowledge, sight, doubt, obedience, truth, and interpretation?*** **WHY IT MUST EVENTUALLY BE OPENED: the objection *"but these things are received by faith — why demand this degree of textual demonstration?"* is reasonable and must be answered rather than dismissed. ❗ BUT NEITHER MAY "FAITH" BECOME AN ESCAPE HATCH WHENEVER EVIDENCE BECOMES UNCOMFORTABLE.** **DISTINCTIONS TO TEST, NOT ASSUME: **faith WITHOUT PRESENT SIGHT** vs **faith WITHOUT GROUNDS** vs **faith CONTRARY TO EVIDENCE** — three categories Scripture may not treat as one.** **⚠️ AND DO NOT ASSUME THE MODERN FAITH/EVIDENCE DICHOTOMY IS THE DISTINCTION SCRIPTURE MAKES. That is itself a candidate anachronism (Anachronism Test).** **LIKELY TEXTS: Hebrews 11 *(esp. 11:1, and the catalogue of people who ACTED on what Yah had revealed — Abraham did not believe without grounds; Yah spoke and he trusted)* · James 2 · Romans 4 and 10 · John 20 (Thomas) · **1 Corinthians 15's WITNESS TRADITION — the apostles appeal to witnesses rather than saying "there is no evidence, believe anyway"** · plus a substantial Tanakh foundation.** **📌 ALREADY PARTIALLY AUDITED: **Hebrews 11:35 was hit at Stage 47 and produced a TRANSLATION ARTIFACT** — ESV renders **κρείττονος ἀναστάσεως** as "a better **LIFE**" rather than "a better **RESURRECTION**" (q229). The branch's key chapter already has a logged artifact in it.** **📌 AND IT INHERITS A PRECEDENT RATHER THAN STARTING BLANK: the methodology's **"Satan Deceives" Is Not Evidence** rule (v2.0) already blocks the identical shape — *I believe X → contrary evidence → the enemy lies → therefore the contradiction proves X.* **The sharpest form of the faith objection this project faces is not the generic one but: *"you are treating an unfalsifiable claim as though evidence could settle it."*** That rule is the answer's foundation.** **🆕 AND THE RULE THAT GOES IN *NOW*, AHEAD OF THE BRANCH, because the objection can arrive at any time: **"I accept Scripture by faith" and "MY INTERPRETATION of Scripture must be accepted by faith" are TWO COMPLETELY DIFFERENT PROPOSITIONS. An interpretation is not shielded from textual examination by being held sincerely.**** **⏸️ EVIDENCE ALREADY DEPOSITED FOR IT: **2 Thessalonians 2:10–12** — ✅ Paul ties belief to **ἀλήθεια** ("the love of the truth," "believe the truth") and unbelief to **ἀδικία**, NOT to accepting a claim against evidence. **ONE PASSAGE ONLY — NO EPISTEMOLOGY MAY BE BUILT FROM IT YET** (Stage 50 ledger, DEFERRED).**

- **⏸️ QUEUED TRANCHES — ARRIVED, DELIBERATELY NOT INGESTED. (recorded 2026-08-24 on the author's instruction to close the archival backlog first.)** **RECORDED HERE SO A CRASH CANNOT TURN "WE DISCUSSED IT" INTO "WE NO LONGER KNOW WHAT SURVIVED."** **STAGE 50½ — *What Temple Did Paul Mean?*: ναός vs ἱερόν across Paul, the NT and the LXX; the Danielic self-exaltation background (Daniel 11:36); the audience-relevance consideration at 2:5; the Future-Sanctuary Brake **already written as q267**. **BLOCKED ON q265's RETRIEVAL.**** **STAGE 51 — *Daniel → Antiochus → Yeshua → Paul: Does the Abomination Pattern Repeat?*: THREE LAYERS to keep separate — **(1)** Daniel itself · **(2)** the **Antiochus IV Epiphanes / Maccabean crisis as a HISTORICAL CONTROL CASE** *(what actually constituted the abomination — a person? an altar? an idol? a cult? the whole event?)* · **(3)** why first-century writers still apply Danielic language **AFTER Antiochus was long dead**. Plus the **SYNOPTIC CONTROL**: Matthew's **τὸ βδέλυγμα τῆς ἐρημώσεως** against **Luke's "Jerusalem surrounded by armies."** Plus a **CANONICAL DISTINCTION owed: Tanakh vs LXX manuscript traditions vs later Christian canons**, with **1–2 Maccabees handled as an EXTRABIBLICAL WITNESS under the Enoch Protocol — never as biblical exegesis** — and ✅ **John 10:22's τὰ ἐγκαίνια (the Feast of Dedication) as the GNT's own acknowledgement of the resulting festival.**** **📌 AND A CLARIFICATION RECORDED NOW TO PREVENT A CONFLATION LATER: **"HEROD'S TEMPLE" DENOTES THE FIRST-CENTURY SECOND-TEMPLE SANCTUARY COMPLEX AS RENOVATED BY HEROD. IT DOES *NOT* IDENTIFY HEROD AS DANIEL'S ABOMINATION, PAUL'S LAWLESS ONE, OR ANY DESECRATING FIGURE.**** **⚠️ AND THE BRAKE THAT MUST TRAVEL INTO STAGE 51: **DO NOT ASSUME ANTIOCHUS IS "MERELY A PARTIAL FULFILMENT."** Determine what Daniel FITS Antiochus, what DOES NOT, and above all **WHY YESHUA COULD STILL POINT HIS DISCIPLES TOWARD DANIEL AFTERWARD.** *Fulfillment ≠ Exhaustion applies — AND its bridge requirement: a second realization needs a TEXTUAL BRIDGE, and here **Yeshua himself may be it**, which would make this the strongest warranted instance the project has seen.*

    - **ADDITIONAL STAGE 51 MATERIAL, ARRIVED 2026-08-24, ALSO NOT INGESTED.** **🔑 THE DECOMPOSITION — the phrase "abomination of desolation" is to be taken apart rather than treated as a title: **ABOMINATION** *(what is profaned or usurped?)* → **SELF-EXALTATION** *(who claims honour belonging to Yah?)* → **SANCTUARY** *(what happens to the holy place and its worship?)* → **DESOLATION** *(what becomes ruined, abandoned, or deprived of proper worship?)* → **DIVINE JUDGMENT** *(what happens to the profaning power?)*. **Run that template through Daniel → Antiochus → Yeshua → Paul, and ONLY THEN compare the Roman/Jerusalem events.** ⚠️ **Daniel uses the expression MORE THAN ONCE AND NOT IDENTICALLY — Hebrew/Aramaic and Greek forms must be compared passage by passage.**** **⚠️ AND A SYNOPTIC PRECISION TO PRESERVE: Luke 21:20 says the armies mean **her desolation HAS DRAWN NEAR** — ❗ **it does NOT say the armies ARE the abomination.** That distinction may matter a great deal, and the two must not be collapsed.** **📌 A SELF-EXALTATION MOTIF WORTH TESTING PAUL'S LANGUAGE AGAINST — *and each of these is a DIFFERENT event, not a chain of identifications:* **Nebuchadnezzar** (Daniel 4 — boasts of his own power and glory, humbled to beastlike existence, ❗ **then lifts his eyes to heaven, acknowledges the Most High, and is RESTORED**) · **Belshazzar** (Daniel 5 — ❗ **profanes the SACRED VESSELS TAKEN FROM THE JERUSALEM TEMPLE in an idolatrous feast**, writing on the wall, killed that night — **conceptually the closest of the three to sanctuary-profanation**) · **Herod Agrippa I** (Acts 12:20–23 — accepts "the voice of a god," struck, **γενόμενος σκωληκόβρωτος**, "becoming worm-eaten") · against **Paul's lawless one** (✅ 2 Thess 2:4 — ἀντικείμενος καὶ ὑπεραιρόμενος… ἀποδεικνύντα ἑαυτὸν ὅτι ἔστιν θεός).** **🛑 AND THE BRAKE TRAVELS WITH IT: **A RECURRING MOTIF DOES NOT ESTABLISH IDENTITY.** Nebuchadnezzar ≠ Belshazzar ≠ Antiochus ≠ Herod Agrippa ≠ the lawless one. The motif supplies a **CONCEPTUAL CATEGORY AGAINST WHICH PAUL'S LANGUAGE CAN BE TESTED** — nothing more. *(Partial-Parallel Brake, Side Branch 34½: shared features do not establish typological correspondence when the DECISIVE MOVEMENT differs — and note that **Nebuchadnezzar's arc RESOLVES IN REPENTANCE AND RESTORATION**, which is precisely the kind of resolution-difference that brake exists to catch.)*** **⚠️ THREE MEMORY CORRECTIONS TO CARRY INTO THE STAGE, none yet verified by this archive: the **beastlike humiliation is Nebuchadnezzar (Daniel 4)**, the **Temple-vessel profanation is Belshazzar (Daniel 5)**, and the **worms are Herod Agrippa I (Acts 12)** — three different rulers in two different books plus Acts. **ALL THREE ARE REPORTED, NOT RETRIEVED. Verify before use.**

Distinct from the numbered list above: those are things actually researched that remain unresolved. These are topics explicitly deferred — noted so nothing gets lost, but genuinely not yet examined. Move an item out of this section (into the numbered list above, or into [[01-convictions]]/[[03-hypotheses]]) once real investigation happens — don't let it sit here pretending to be settled just because it's written down.

- **Luke 1:3's "orderly" (Greek *kathexēs*) doesn't necessarily mean strict chronological order.** (Flagged 2026-08-09, see [[00b-biblical-epistemology]] "Luke's Own Method.") Relevant later when comparing the Gospels' sometimes-differing event sequences — don't assume "orderly account" makes Luke's sequence the chronological arbiter against Matthew's or Mark's without checking what *kathexēs* actually claims.
- ~~**The trumpet comparison — one event or independent uses of shared imagery?**~~ **→ PROMOTED to numbered item 239 on 2026-08-23.** Investigated at Stage 48 ([[02n-the-gathering-identity-audit]]). **Partially resolved — LAST ≠ SEVENTH refused; still blocked on the LXX of Isaiah 27:13 (q231).**
- **Does Revelation inherit Isaiah 27's serpent-defeat/restoration pattern?** (Flagged 2026-08-11, see [[00k-the-gathering-of-the-elect]] §8.) Isaiah 27:1 has Leviathan/the dragon judged, then vineyard restored, Israel gathered, great trumpet. Revelation has dragon/ancient serpent → defeat → Messiah's authority → the people of Yahweh. Recorded as an intertextual observation, deliberately unresolved.
- ~~**Who are "the elect" in Matthew 24:31?**~~ **→ PROMOTED to numbered item 240 on 2026-08-23.** Re-opened at Stage 48 and **STILL UNRESOLVED** — but Matthew 23:37's *τὰ τέκνα σου* now pulls it toward the covenantal reading (q236).
- **Yeshua's departure and Ezekiel's glory.** (Flagged 2026-08-10, see [[00j-cosmic-signs-and-intertextual-priority]] §8.) Ezekiel 11:23 has Yahweh's glory leaving the condemned city and stopping on "the mountain east of the city" — the Mount of Olives. Matthew 23:38 declares the house desolate, 24:1 has Yeshua leaving the Temple, 24:3 places Him on the Mount of Olives. **Evidentiary status: supports** (stronger than permits, well short of requires). Matthew never states the fulfillment; the pattern is striking and the authorial intent unstated.
- **Danielic Kingdom Parallel — Daniel 2, the stone, and when Messiah's reign begins.** (Flagged 2026-08-12.) **Deliberately NOT used as evidence in [[00j-cosmic-signs-and-intertextual-priority]]**, because Daniel 2 does not supply Matthew 24's cosmic-luminary vocabulary. Inserting it there merely because it also concerns kingdoms and judgment would have made that argument *less* precise. **This illustrates something the method should keep:** a passage can be true and enormously relevant to the larger theology while not being relevant evidence for the specific exegetical question currently under investigation. We are not collecting verses that broadly agree — we are asking each text a specific question.
    - **What Daniel 2 textually establishes:** four successive kingdoms (gold head = Babylon, explicitly, 2:37–38; then silver, bronze, iron) → the fourth "strong as iron" (2:40) → feet/toes "**a divided kingdom**, partly strong and partly brittle," which "will not hold together, just as iron does not mix with clay" (2:41–43) → a stone "**cut out by no human hand**" strikes the feet, the whole image is pulverized, and the stone "became a great mountain and **filled the whole earth**" (2:34–35) → "**in the days of those kings** the God of heaven will set up a kingdom that shall never be destroyed" (2:44).
    - **Traditional identification** (substantial pedigree, not uncontested): Babylon → Medo-Persia → Greece → Rome. Strengthened by **Daniel 8:20–21**, which explicitly names "the kings of Media and Persia" and "the king of Greece." **Competing scholarly reconstructions exist** — notably ones separating Media from Persia and making Greece the fourth kingdom — and should be documented rather than ignored.
    - **Corrections recorded (2026-08-12):** (1) The sequence has **four** kingdoms, not five — separating "Medes" and "Persians" would give five. (2) Daniel does **not** identify the iron/clay phase as modern governments descended from Rome; that belongs to later eschatological systems, not the Biblical Observation bucket. (3) Nor does Daniel identify it as Republic vs. Empire — and for the record, **the Roman Republic preceded the Empire** (conventionally 509–27 BC, then Augustus from 27 BC), so any such reconstruction would need its phases stated in the right order. The text says: divided, partly iron, partly brittle, alliances that don't adhere. Everything past that is interpretation.
    - **The genuinely productive intertextual question this raises:** Daniel 2 gives *four kingdoms → an everlasting divine kingdom* (2:44). Daniel 7 gives *four beasts → judgment → Son of Man → everlasting dominion* (7:13–14). Same endpoint, different symbolic register. And Daniel 2:44 places the kingdom's establishment "**in the days of those kings**" — not millennia after the fourth kingdom vanished. Yeshua then appears under Roman rule announcing **Mark 1:15**: ESV: "**The time is fulfilled**, and the kingdom of God is at hand." CJB: "The time has come, God's Kingdom is near!"
    - **And the stone imagery recurs in Matthew.** **Matthew 21:42–44** — quoting Psalm 118's rejected stone, then: ESV: "the one who falls on this stone will be broken to pieces; and when it falls on anyone, **it will crush him**." RVR1960: "sobre quien ella cayere, **le desmenuzará**" — the same crushing/pulverizing language Daniel 2 uses of the stone striking the image. **And Matthew 21 sits immediately before the Matthew 23–24 judgment material this framework has been working.** *(Textual note: some manuscripts omit Matthew 21:44 — CJB's rendering ends at v.43. Worth verifying before building on it.)*
    - **Open Intertextual Question:** what relationship exists among Daniel 2's stone kingdom, Daniel 7's Son of Man kingdom, Psalm 118's rejected stone, Matthew 21:42–44's crushing stone, and Yeshua's announcement that the kingdom had drawn near?
    - **The larger question it feeds:** before asking *when did the thousand years begin*, this framework must first establish **when Scripture says Messiah's reign began**. Those are not necessarily the same question, and collapsing them prematurely would be exactly the error the method exists to prevent.
- **Open hypothesis — Rupes Nigra and Daniel 2:35.** (Raised 2026-08-12.) Daniel 2:34–35's stone "cut out by no human hand" becoming "a great mountain" that "filled the whole earth" prompted a connection to later cartographic traditions concerning **Rupes Nigra**, depicted as a great magnetic black rock/mountain near the geographic north.
    - **No identification between Daniel's mountain and Rupes Nigra is established.** Compound hypothesis requiring several independent propositions to survive: (a) what Daniel's mountain means textually, (b) the provenance and development of the Rupes Nigra cartographic tradition, (c) any connection between them.
    - **Method requirement:** investigate Daniel 2 on its own, investigate Rupes Nigra on its own, **then** compare. Do not research Rupes Nigra *in order to* prove Daniel 2 — that inverts the epistemological loop.
    - **Lexical note:** Daniel 2 is **Aramaic** at this point, not Hebrew. 2:35's "whole earth" is **כָּל־אַרְעָא** (*kol-ʾarʿāʾ*). Conclusions reached about Greek *gē* or *oikoumenē* ([[00g-matthew-24-and-the-oikoumene]], [[00l-tribes-of-the-land]]) **cannot be imported** — *ʾarʿāʾ* needs its own study within Biblical Aramaic. Same principle, different language.
    - **Brake on the magnetism argument:** compass needles align with Earth's magnetic field; they are not attracted toward a magnetic mountain at a geographic center. "Compasses point north → therefore Rupes Nigra" is not usable evidence. *(Term note: the field is described as **toroidal**, not "Horus.")*
    - **Brake on the cartographic argument:** old European maps genuinely did depict a great Arctic magnetic rock, and the **tradition itself is a legitimate historical research subject** — how old are the surviving sources, where did the story originate, what did they actually claim, were cartographers reporting observation or reproducing inherited lore? **Critical condition:** independent maps only count as independent evidence if they are not all copying one earlier source. Twenty maps reproducing one tradition are one witness, not twenty. Belongs in [[02-historical-observations]] when earned.
- **The Cross as Cosmic Turning Point.** (Flagged 2026-08-10, see [[00i-stars-the-dragon-and-the-cross]] §7 for the assembled arc.) Job's accuser with heavenly access → John 12:31 "now will the ruler of this world be cast out" → the cross → the torn veil → Colossians 2:15's disarmed rulers → Hebrews 10:19–22's new access → Revelation 12:10–11's dragon cast down and conquered "by the blood of the Lamb." Deserves its own treatment; possibly also Ephesians 4. The *connection* is textually supported; the *chronology* of Revelation 12's heavenly war relative to the cross still needs testing.
- **Melchizedek: Messiah as King-Priest.** (Flagged 2026-08-10, sources captured in [[00i-stars-the-dragon-and-the-cross]] §8.) Genesis 14:18–20, Psalm 110:1+4, Hebrews 5–7, Zechariah 6:12–13. Includes the pre-Levitical priesthood question (Exodus 19:22, 24:5; Jethro) and the caution that Abraham's tenth predates Sinai without thereby establishing a universal tithe command.
- **"The powers of the heavens" (Matthew 24:29) — what are they?** (Flagged 2026-08-10, see [[00j-cosmic-signs-and-intertextual-priority]] §9.) Greek *hai dynameis tōn ouranōn*. Live possibilities: celestial bodies, heavenly/spiritual powers, symbolic ruling structures, cosmic order generally. Revelation 12's heavenly-beings material makes the spiritual reading live; Isaiah 13/34 and Ezekiel 32 equally support cosmic-judgment imagery. Needs the phrase examined elsewhere in Scripture before deciding. *(The broader falling-stars question is now largely worked in 00j — this specific phrase is what remains open from it.)*
- **New Testament use of the Septuagint where it differs from the Masoretic Text.** (Flagged 2026-08-09, see [[00c-commission-covenant-investigation]] Acts 15.) James's citation of Amos 9:11–12 in Acts 15:16–18 follows the Greek Septuagint tradition more closely than the later Masoretic Hebrew wording. **This directly complicates ADR-0003's "go to the source language" rule** — the apostles themselves sometimes quote the Greek Scriptures circulating in their world, so "go back to the Hebrew" is not always the simple corrective it sounds like. Needs real work: which text-form did NT authors use, when, and what follows for a study that treats original-language priority as a methodological principle. Not resolved; genuinely important for the translation policy.
- **Independent testimony and variation in detail.** (Flagged 2026-08-09.) General historical principle worth stating carefully rather than loosely: independent witnesses don't need identical wording to be credible, and perfectly harmonized accounts can suggest dependence rather than independence. But variation isn't automatically proof of authenticity either — each discrepancy still needs its own examination. Proper formulation to work from: *agreement in core events + independent variation in detail is consistent with genuine testimony, but every specific discrepancy still requires examination.* Belongs with the Luke-as-historian material ([[00b-biblical-epistemology]], "Luke's Own Method") and any future Gospel-comparison work — not developed yet.
- **Circumcision of the heart, and whether Gentile believers required physical circumcision.** (Flagged 2026-08-09.) This is precisely the question Acts 15 forces into the open — whether Gentile men entering Messiah had to be circumcised and take on Mosaic covenant markers. The Jerusalem council's ruling (Acts 15:19–20, already cited in [[00c-commission-covenant-investigation]]) addresses it, but the fuller "circumcision of the heart" thread (Deuteronomy 10:16, Deuteronomy 30:6, Jeremiah 4:4, Romans 2:28–29, Colossians 2:11) hasn't been traced. Next in sequence — Acts 13–15 is where this gets worked.
- **The Sinai/Horeb naming question.** (Noted 2026-08-09, minor.) The mountain of the Ten Commandments is **Sinai**; "Horeb" is the closely-related name used more often in Deuteronomy, with "Sinai" more common in Exodus. Not two different mountains in the biblical tradition. *(Separately, and unrelated to the naming: claims relocating Sinai outside the conventional region belong in [[03-hypotheses]] item 10, alternative geography — not here.)*
- **"The Way" (ἡ ὁδός, *hē hodos*) as the movement's earliest self-description.** (Flagged 2026-08-09, see [[00c-commission-covenant-investigation]] Acts 9.) Acts uses it repeatedly (9:2, and elsewhere) before "Christian" appears at Antioch (11:26). Worth tracing all occurrences and asking what the term itself claims — relevant to the naming/label discussion in [[01-convictions]] B.5, but not yet investigated.
- **Messiah identifying Himself with His people.** (Flagged 2026-08-09, see [[00c-commission-covenant-investigation]] Acts 9.) Acts 9:4 — Saul is arresting Yeshua's followers, and Yeshua asks "why are you persecuting **me**?" Relevant to covenant-identity and body-of-Messiah material; connects to Romans 11's olive tree and Ephesians 2's "one new man," neither developed yet.
- **Acts 11:20 textual variant — *Hellēnistas* vs. *Hellēnas*.** (Flagged 2026-08-09.) Manuscripts differ between "Hellenists" (Greek-speaking Jews) and "Greeks" (Gentiles). Real consequences for how large a step Acts 11:20 records — if Hellenists, the Antioch believers were still within Israel; if Greeks, this is a Gentile-mission datapoint independent of Cornelius. Needs manuscript-evidence work per ADR-0003's source-language priority; not resolved.
- **Romans 9–11 on Israel's ongoing place.** (Flagged 2026-08-09, see [[00c-commission-covenant-investigation]] Acts 9.) Acts 9:15 keeps "the children of Israel" inside Paul's commission alongside Gentiles and kings — the shape is not *Israel discarded, Gentiles substituted*. Romans 9–11 addresses this directly and will have to be worked through; deliberately not touched yet.
- **"Last days" as a first-century chronological marker.** (Flagged 2026-08-09, see [[00c-commission-covenant-investigation]] §1, Acts 2 section.) Peter applies Joel's "last days" prophecy (Acts 2:16–17) to his own first-century audience, not a future era. Needs comparison with Hebrews 1:1–2 ("in these last days he has spoken to us by his Son"), 1 Peter 1:20 ("manifest in the last times for the sake of you"), Hebrews 9:26 ("at the end of the ages"), and 1 Corinthians 10:11 ("on whom the end of the ages has come") — all apostolic-era authors placing "last days"/"end of the ages" language on their own generation. Directly relevant to the preterist timeline question this whole framework circles back to — not touched yet, deliberately deferred until the direct return to [[06-revelation-19-22-timeline]].
- **Ezekiel 40–48 — the temple vision.** (Flagged 2026-08-12, see [[00o-new-covenant-what-changed]] §7 and numbered item 19.) Nine chapters of detailed temple, priesthood, sacrifice, and land-allotment specification, given *after* Ezekiel 37's everlasting-covenant promise. **Deliberately not opened at Stage 15** — it is a large investigation and cashing it mid-stage would repeat the error the Daniel 2 discipline exists to prevent. Directly governs item 19's sanctuary question and cannot be settled without it. Note in advance: the vision includes **sin offerings** (Ezekiel 43–45), which any "sacrifice fully and finally ended" reading must address rather than skip — flagged now so a later session cannot quietly avoid it.
- **Leviticus 16 — the Day of Atonement, to be read BEFORE Hebrews' interpretation of it.** (Flagged 2026-08-12, see [[00o-new-covenant-what-changed]] §18.) Two goats, the scapegoat sent away, the annual repetition, the high priest's own sin offering first. **Sequencing constraint, not a preference:** reading Hebrews 7–10 first and Leviticus second would guarantee finding exactly what Hebrews said was there. The next stage's whole value depends on getting this order right.
- **2 Corinthians 6:16's apparent citation of Ezekiel 37:27.** (Flagged 2026-08-12.) If Paul does cite Ezekiel's "my dwelling place shall be with them" and apply it to the believing community, it is directly relevant to item 19's sanctuary question. **NOT VERIFIED by this project** — the text has not been fetched and the citation not confirmed. Do not cite it as evidence until it has been.
- **Genesis 7 — the clean/unclean animal distinction predates Moses.** (Flagged 2026-08-12, see [[00q-circumcision-covenant-sign-and-heart]] §23.) Noah is instructed regarding clean and unclean animals before Sinai exists. **This is the second consecutive institution found to predate Sinai** (after circumcision). Directly governs the next stage on food. **Larger question surfaced and deliberately carried forward rather than answered:** if both circumcision *and* clean/unclean predate Sinai, **how much of what is casually called "the Mosaic Law" actually originates there at all?** Not the next stage's question, but now visible and not to be lost.
- **1 Enoch's manuscript status by section — Qumran Aramaic vs. later Ethiopic.** (Flagged 2026-08-12, see [[00-methodology-current]] §Extrabiblical Source Protocol.) **1 Enoch is not one manuscript from one moment in time.** Portions have Aramaic Dead Sea Scroll witnesses; others depend primarily on later Ethiopic transmission. Before any Enochic claim is used as evidence about Second Temple belief, its section's manuscript status must be established. Governs the Azazel branch (item 22) — the ch. 6/8/10 material is now verified *as Charles's text*, but its **manuscript-language status has not been checked**.
